### [1] [Microcomb spectral fingerprinting enables parts-per-trillion methane detection with low humidity cross-sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24338)
*Shiyan Cao,Yinchao Xu,Judith Su*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出集成微梳传感架构，在单一超高Q值二氧化硅微谐振腔中统一共振增强倏逝场传感与多波长分子指纹识别，以甲烷为模型实现178 ppt检测限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光频梳传统上仅用作探测外部样品的光源，依赖分立的长光程吸收池；如何在紧凑平台上同时获得高灵敏与化学特异性识别仍具挑战。

Method: 构建超高Q值二氧化硅微谐振腔微梳，气态分析物直接与腔内循环光的倏逝场相互作用；不依赖单一共振，而利用分布于多条梳齿上的集体光谱响应生成多维分子指纹，并系统增加梳齿数目以评估判别能力。

Result: 甲烷检测限估计为178 ppt；对氨、一氧化碳、己烷及相对湿度90%以下的水汽光谱响应极小；增加梳齿数可系统性提升分析物判别能力，显示微梳提供超越单一光谱通道的传感信息。

Conclusion: 以超高Q微腔中的共振光循环替代物理长光程，将紧凑光学传感推进至parts-per-trillion量级，并利用分布式光谱信息实现化学识别。

Abstract: Optical frequency combs have transformed molecular spectroscopy by enabling broadband, high-resolution analysis, yet they are conventionally used as light sources to interrogate external samples. Here, we introduce an integrated microcomb sensing architecture that unifies resonantly enhanced evanescent sensing with multiwavelength molecular fingerprinting in a single ultrahigh-quality-factor silica microresonator. Gaseous analytes interact directly with the evanescent field of light recirculating within the resonator, enhancing sensitivity while encoding analyte-induced perturbations across the microcomb's collective spectral response. Rather than relying on a single optical resonance, we exploit distributed responses across multiple comb lines to generate multidimensional molecular fingerprints. Adding more comb lines systematically improves analyte discrimination, showing that the microcomb provides sensing information beyond what individual spectral channels offer. Using methane as a model analyte, we achieve an estimated detection limit of 178 parts per trillion, with minimal spectral responses to ammonia, carbon monoxide, hexane, and water vapor at relative humidities up to 90%. By replacing physically long interaction paths with resonant light recirculation in an ultrahigh-Q microresonator, this architecture brings compact optical sensing into the parts-per-trillion regime while exploiting distributed spectral information for chemical identification.

</details>


### [2] [Freeform gradient-index media reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24393)
*V. Chávez-Islas,F. H. Maldonado-Villamizar,E. Espíndola-Ramos,S. Chávez-Cerda,J. E. Gómez-Correa*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出一种三维逐点重建自由曲面GRIN介质的逆问题方法，通过局部折射的逆应用实现，无需几何对称性、傍轴近似或等折射率面先验知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GRIN介质重建方法依赖几何对称性与射线守恒量，难以推广到自由曲面（freeform）介质，因此需要不依赖对称性的重建方法。

Method: 基于局部折射定律的逆应用，构建三维逐点（point-wise）重建框架，逐点确定折射率分布以匹配给定射线轨迹，不要求几何对称、傍轴近似或等折射率面先验信息，实现简单。

Result: 以两种界面几何进行重建，表明同一给定轨迹函数可对应不同的折射率分布；界面选择因此为光学设计提供额外自由度，但受局部折射条件与重叠重建区域间相容性约束。

Conclusion: 该逐点逆折射方法将GRIN重建推广至自由曲面介质，并把界面几何作为设计自由度，为自由曲面GRIN光学设计提供了可行且易实现的途径。

Abstract: Reconstructing a gradient-index (GRIN) medium consists of determining a spatial refractive index distribution that produces a prescribed ray trajectory, an inverse problem in geometrical optics. Previous approaches have used geometrical symmetries and conserved ray quantities to address this inverse problem. Extending reconstruction to freeform media requires approaches that do not rely on these symmetries. Here, we present a three-dimensional point-wise method for reconstructing freeform GRIN media through the inverse application of local refraction. The method does not require geometrical symmetry, paraxial approximations, or prior knowledge of the iso-indicial surfaces, and is straightforward to implement. Reconstruction using two interface geometries demonstrates that different refractive index distributions can be obtained from the same prescribed trajectory function. The choice of interfaces thus provides an additional degree of freedom for optical design, subject to local refraction conditions and compatibility between overlapping reconstructed regions.

</details>


### [3] [Charge-Selective Displacement of Spatiotemporal Optical Vortices at a Spatially Uniform Time Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24713)
*Chao Mei,Peilong Yang*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 研究时空光涡旋（STOV）在空间均匀、面内各向同性时间边界处的横向位移：位移随拓扑电荷变号，但转换权重与电荷无关；径向谱滤波使位移遵循谱权重的对数斜率且横向平均动量为零；在泵浦GaAs波导中提取过程的非对称衰减带来额外角偏转（探测器滤波前约±42 mrad）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究STOV在时间边界（折射率/介质的瞬时变化）下的横向位移规律，厘清其电荷依赖性与光谱滤波的耦合机制，并区分真实位移与传播、提取过程引入的伪效应。

Method: 理论分析空间均匀、面内各向同性时间边界处的STOV横向位移与转换权重；引入径向谱滤波分析其与涡旋相位的耦合；对窄带标量高斯谱推导位移与谱权重对数斜率的关系；在有限长泵浦GaAs波导中考虑提取阶段的不均匀衰减；并用独立的Maxwell–Drude数值模拟检验提取诱导响应，同时在多个平面上计算质心轨迹以分离传播漂移与位置偏移。

Result: 横向位移随拓扑电荷改变符号，而转换权重与电荷无关；相反的电荷经径向滤波后转换谱强度相同；窄带高斯谱下位移等于谱权重的对数斜率，且横向平均动量为零；GaAs波导中提取期的不均匀衰减产生额外角偏转，Maxwell–Drude模拟给出探测器滤波前约±42 mrad；多平面质心轨迹可将传播诱导漂移与位置偏移区分开。

Conclusion: 时间边界处STOV的横向位移由涡旋相位与谱权重共同决定，具有电荷反对称性和零横向动量的特征；实际波导中提取过程会叠加角偏转，需通过多平面质心分析与数值模拟加以甄别。

Abstract: At a spatially uniform, in-plane-isotropic time boundary, the transverse displacement of a spatiotemporal optical vortex changes sign with its topological charge, despite a charge-independent conversion weight. Radial spectral filtering couples to the vortex phase while preserving identical converted spectral intensities for opposite charges. For a narrow scalar Gaussian spectrum, the displacement follows the logarithmic slope of the spectral weight and occurs with zero mean transverse momentum. In a finite pumped GaAs waveguide, unequal attenuation during extraction adds angular deflection. Independent Maxwell--Drude simulations test this extraction-induced response and give $\pm42$~mrad before detector filtering. Calculated centroid traces at multiple planes separate propagation-induced drift from position offsets.

</details>


### [4] [Realization of valley-controlled orbital angular momentum via inter-orbital coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24714)
*Wenxia Xu,Ziteng Wang,Yihan Wang,Qinggang Shang,Liqin Tang,Jingjun Xu,Hrvoje Buljan,Daohong Song,Zhigang Chen*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该论文在破缺反演对称性的光子蜂窝晶格中实验观测到谷控制的轨道角动量（OAM）态，OAM由轨道间耦合产生，其拓扑荷符号由谷自由度（K/K'）决定，实现 vortex 手性的确定性调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OAM是理解固体与人工晶格中新奇现象的基本自由度，但在晶格体系中OAM的产生与操控仍具挑战，尤其在缺乏对OAM自由度的有效调控手段时。

Method: 构建含 s 轨道与高轨道子晶格、反演对称性破缺的光子蜂窝晶格；在K或K'谷选择性激发无初始OAM的s轨道模式，通过s-p与s-d轨道间耦合产生OAM态；并结合理论分析 vortex 手性对称性。

Result: 实时空间呈现手性涡旋阵列；s-p耦合产生拓扑荷l=±1，s-d耦合产生l=±2；拓扑荷符号由谷自由度决定，实现全阵列涡旋手性的确定性控制；理论揭示局域涡旋手性对称性强制K与K'谷的谷依赖手性选择。

Conclusion: 建立了通过轨道物理工程手性涡旋阵列的通用平台，为光子体系及其他体系中轨道与拓扑介导现象的研究提供新途径。

Abstract: Orbital angular momentum (OAM) represents a fundamental degree of freedom (DoF) for understanding novel phenomena in solids and synthetic lattices. However, the generation and control of OAM in lattice systems remain challenging. Here, we experimentally observe valley-controlled OAM states in inversion-symmetry-broken photonic honeycomb lattices consisting of s- and higher-orbital sublattices. These OAM states manifest as chiral vortex arrays in real space. By selectively exciting s-orbital modes carrying no initial OAM at either the K or K' valley, we observe higher-orbital OAM modes generated via inter-orbital coupling in neighboring sites. Specifically, s-p orbital coupling generates OAM states with topological charge l=+1 or l=-1, while s-d orbital coupling yields higher-charge OAM states with l=+2 or l=-2. The sign of the topological charge is determined by the valley DoF, enabling deterministic control of vortex chirality across the entire array. Our theory further reveals that a local vortex-chirality symmetry enforces the valley-dependent selection of vortex chirality at the K and K' valleys, establishing a direct connection between the valley DoF and real-space vortex chirality. These results establish a versatile platform for engineering chiral vortex arrays via orbital physics and exploring orbital- and topology-mediated phenomena in photonic systems and beyond.

</details>


### [5] [Boosting fluorescence and polarization with nanophotonics for single-molecule chiral sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24746)
*Ershad Mohammadi,T. V. Raziman,Alberto G. Curto*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出基于高折射率介质纳米光子学的圆偏振荧光增强框架，实现荧光强度千倍增强与偏振度十倍提升，为单分子手性传感提供可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 圆二色性依赖圆偏振光差分吸收，但低浓度下吸收趋近于零导致灵敏度受限；荧光方法虽可达到单分子极限，却受弱荧光信号与低偏振对比度双重制约。

Method: 利用高折射率介质纳米光子结构同时增强荧光与偏振；建立基于互易原理的解析框架，描述手性偶极发射体与纳米天线的耦合，剖析圆偏振荧光增强的各贡献项与权衡关系；据此设计非手性介质纳米结构。

Result: 定制化非手性介质纳米结构实现荧光强度约1000倍增强、偏振度约10倍提升；并证实在纳米结构角度发射图样中必须考虑外禀偏振效应，否则无法可靠区分对映体。

Conclusion: 该工作为单分子手性传感开辟了现实可行路径，并强调外禀偏振效应在纳米光子手性检测中的不可或缺性。

Abstract: Detecting chirality is essential in chemistry and biomedicine. Circular dichroism identifies chiral molecules through their differential absorption of circularly polarized light. However, its sensitivity is limited by vanishing absorption at low concentrations. Fluorescence-based methods have already enabled other optical techniques to reach the single-molecule limit and could, in principle, also extend chiral detection to reach this sensitivity regime. They are marred, however, by the combination of weak fluorescence signals and low polarization contrast. To overcome these limitations, we leverage high-index dielectric nanophotonics to simultaneously enhance both fluorescence and polarization for chiral sensing. We propose a reciprocity-based analytical framework for a chiral dipolar emitter coupled to a nanoantenna and reveal the different contributions and trade-offs governing the enhancement of circularly polarized fluorescence. Guided by this insight, we tailor achiral dielectric nanostructures to reach a 1000-fold enhancement of fluorescence intensity together with a 10-fold increase in polarization. Crucially, we show that accounting for extrinsic polarization effects in the angular emission patterns of nanophotonic structures is indispensable for reliable enantiomer discrimination. Our approach opens a realistic path for single-molecule chiral sensing.

</details>


### [6] [Dynamic Hotspot Migration and Self-Induced Resonances in Ultrafast Laser-Irradiated Silicon Thin Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25079)
*George D. Tsibidis,Yi Wang,Emmanuel Stratakis*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 硅薄膜中载流子诱导折射率调制与自诱导Fabry-Pérot共振的相互作用决定了飞秒激光能量空间分布，并揭示非线性热点迁移与共振吸收到等离子体屏蔽的转变机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 飞秒激光加工硅薄膜时，能量在深度方向如何分布缺乏清晰物理图像，尤其载流子诱导折射率变化与薄膜内干涉共振的耦合作用未被充分阐明，制约了深度分辨的激光纳米制造。

Method: 针对100、230、350 nm三种厚度的SOI构型进行数值分析，考察载流子诱导折射率调制与自诱导Fabry-Pérot共振的耦合，划分界面局域、双节点与多节点三类激发区，并分析高能量密度下的等离子体消光效应。

Result: 识别出界面局域、双节点和多节点三种特征激发区；发现非线性热点迁移效应——折射率瞬态降低使内部驻波拉伸，脉冲期间强度波腹向薄膜体区移动；高注量下等离子体诱导消光抑制内反射，吸收由共振介导转变为等离子体屏蔽。

Conclusion: 建立了激光纳米制造中深度分辨改性的物理基础，具备表面图案化与亚表面深度分辨改性并行的双重能力，可直接应用于激光辅助界面分离与三维集成光子器件制造。

Abstract: The spatial distribution of energy in silicon thin films under femtosecond laser irradiation is demonstrated to be influenced by the interaction between carrier-induced refractive index modulation and self-induced Fabry-Pérot resonances. We resolve three characteristic excitation regions such as interface-localized, binodal and multi-nodal regimes considering the analysis of silicon-on-insulator (SOI) configurations across thicknesses of 100, 230, and 350 nm. We report a nonlinear hotspot migration effect where the transient reduction of the refractive index stretches the internal standing wave, translating intensity antinodes toward the film volume during the pulse. Numerical results demonstrate that at higher fluences, plasma-induced extinction suppresses internal reflections, triggering a transition from resonance-mediated absorption to plasma shielding. These findings establish a physical basis for depth-resolved modification in laser nanomanufacturing, providing a dual-capacity for simultaneous surface patterning and depth-resolved subsurface modification with direct applications in laser-assisted interfacial separation and 3D integrated photonic fabrication.

</details>


### [7] [Superradiant temporal transition radiation of a relativistic electron beam](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25165)
*Ariel Nause,Avraham Gover*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出并分析时间边界辐射（t-TR）：利用飞秒激光在电子束渡越半导体孔径期间将硅瞬时切换为电子-空穴等离子体，使束缚场成为新介质的初始条件并以等离子体频率辐射；能量随电子数N²标度，FDTD与解析模型一致，预测6.5 MeV装置上可单发探测的可调宽带THz辐射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 匀速运动电荷的束缚场本身不辐射；空间边界（传统渡越辐射）只能释放部分场能。作者希望借助"时间边界"——介质参数在时间上突变——释放束缚场，并利用波矢守恒、频率不守恒的特性，获得束流自身谱不覆盖的辐射频段。

Method: 相对论电子束穿过半导体（Si）晶片上的孔径，束缚场占据晶片时用飞秒激光将硅切换为电子-空穴等离子体，使原场成为新介质的初始条件；推导时间边界的场能转换关系，并计入等离子体载流子碰撞损耗，给出可测振幅与频率线型的简化解析模型及标度律；用FDTD数值计算基于6.5 MeV光阴极注入器参数进行验证。

Result: 解析模型与FDTD结果吻合。参考工况下预测THz波段宽带t-TR辐射：中心频率接近等离子体频率，可由激光通量在一个倍频程内选择，强度由束流电荷控制；所有电子在切换时刻同相贡献，能量按N²标度，单发即可探测。

Conclusion: 时间边界可作为相干、可调、单发可测的THz辐射新机制，其频率由介质切换参数而非束流谱决定；实验观测方案见补充材料。

Abstract: A charge in uniform motion carries a bound field that does not radiate. Transition radiation releases part of it at a boundary in space. We propose and analyze the case of radiation emission due to a boundary in time: a relativistic electron bunch passes through an aperture in a semiconductor (silicon) wafer, and while its bound field occupies the wafer, a femtosecond laser switches the semiconductor into an electron-hole plasma. The field is a valid solution of the old medium and not of the new one, so it is handed to the plasma as an initial condition for radiation emission at the plasma frequency. Because in temporal transition radiation (t-TR) the wavevector is conserved, and the frequency is not, the emission can appear in a spectral band that the bunch's own field does not populate. Every electron contributes in phase to the initial condition at the switching moment, so the emitted energy scales as $N^2$. We derive the field energy conversion at the temporal boundary and the resultant measurable amplitude and frequency line-shape of the radiation taking into consideration plasma carriers collisional loss. The simplified analytical model of the process and its scaling laws are confirmed by use of FDTD numerical computations for a reference case based on the parameters of an existing 6.5 MeV photoinjector accelerator. For this case we predict wideband t-TR radiation emission at THz frequencies, where the emission center frequency, near the plasma frequency, is selectable across an octave, set by the laser fluence, and the intensity is governed by the beam charge. The predicted radiative energy is detectable in a single shot. The experimental considerations for observing it are given in a Supplement.

</details>


### [8] [Design and synthesis of laser inscribed flexible Bragg gratings containing silicon oxynitride-doped silicon thin film for authentication applications with the insights from X-Ray Absorption Spectroscopy and Quantum Mechanical Calculations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25382)
*Ali Karatutlu,Timuçin Emre Tabaru,Umut Taylan,Zehra Gizem Mutlay,Doğukan Hazar Özbey,Okan Uslu,Hamid-Reza Bahari,Esra Kendir Tekgül,Andriy Budnyk,İremnur Duru,Mustafa Fatih Genisel,Zeynep Reyhan Öztürk,Taylan Gorkan,Engin Durgun,Bülend Ortaç*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该研究在低于100°C的柔性衬底上合成掺氧氮化硅的硅（SiON-doped Si），制成布拉格光栅，实现从紫外到近红外多光谱反射，用于保护超快激光刻写微信息，并结合软X射线吸收谱与DFT/粒子群优化揭示其结构与形成机理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SOI是硅光子的主流材料体系，但需拓展新材料以精确调控折射率并赋予光敏性；同时柔性衬底上低温制备光栅对先进应用有需求。

Method: 在<100°C下于柔性衬底合成SiON-doped Si并制备布拉格光栅；利用软X射线吸收谱在Si L、O K、N K吸收边表征结构；用粒子群优化结合DFT进行块体Si3N4-xOx晶体结构预测与量子力学计算。

Result: SiON-doped Si折射率可在2.1–2.5精确调控并具多波长光敏性；BG表现出UV至NIR的多光谱反射；数值与实验均验证其可保护超快激光刻写的微信息。

Conclusion: SiON-doped Si为硅光子学提供新定位，兼容柔性低温工艺，兼具可调折射率与宽谱反射功能。

Abstract: Silicon-on-insulator is mostly the main driving material system for the field of silicon photonics, including PICs, due to high index contrast and compatibility with current semiconductor manufacturing processes [1]. A recent example is silicon oxynitride-doped silicon [2] discovered recently by our group, offering to set a new position for the fields of silicon photonics with a precise control of refractive index from 2.1 to 2.5 together with the photosensitivity property at different wavelengths. In this study, we demonstrate that the synthesis of silicon oxynitride-doped silicon can be performed on a flexible substrate for the fabrication of Bragg gratings (BG) at low temperatures below 100 degrees Celsius that are emerging for advanced applications. Their use in preserving ultra-fast lasers inscribed micro-information has been demonstrated numerically and experimentally due to its multispectral reflection behaviour from the ultraviolet (UV) to the near-infrared (NIR) regions. The unique structural characteristics of silicon oxynitride-doped silicon were studied at the Si L, O K and N K edges using soft X-ray absorption studies. The quantum mechanical calculations provide the potential formation mechanism of SiON-doped Si, whereas the crystal structure prediction for bulk Si3N4-xOx was conducted using the particle swarm optimization method in combination with density functional theory (DFT).

</details>


### [9] [All-Dielectric Tight-Binding Photonics via a Band-Gap Vacuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25655)
*Aodong Li,Jing Li,Tao Xiao,Biye Xie*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 将谐振器嵌入光子带隙真空（BgV）中，用带隙局域化消除空气中辐射尾巴导致的杂散长程耦合，从而在全介质光子晶体中实验实现忠实的紧束缚哈密顿量并演示拓扑相与规范场调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 构建光子带隙真空（BgV）——以光子晶体带隙扮演电子真空的角色，使各谐振模式指数局域，仅保留设计的短程耦合；全介质光子晶体中实现BBH模型并用微波测量解析角态及其轨道；通过调节BgV色散与Bloch模式控制相对耦合与环路干涉；结合全波计算与提取模型，并数值实现三维烧绿石与hyperhoneycomb晶格。

Abstract: Tight-binding photonics bridges condensed-matter physics and optics, providing a powerful framework for engineering band structures and topological phases with light. Here we embed resonators in a photonic band-gap vacuum (BgV), a photonic crystal whose band gap plays for photons the role vacuum plays for bound electrons. The BgV localizes each resonance exponentially, so designed short-range couplings define the lattice Hamiltonian. We thereby experimentally realize a faithful Benalcazar-Bernevig-Hughes Hamiltonian in an all-dielectric photonic crystal, resolving corner resonances and their orbitals in microwave measurements. Unlike the vacuum surrounding electrons, the BgV is itself designable, controlling relative couplings and closed-loop interference through its band dispersion and Bloch modes. Full-wave calculations and extracted models show that uniformly modifying the BgV, with all resonators fixed, switches the quantized polarization of a two-dimensional Su-Schrieffer-Heeger lattice and converts a zero-flux lattice into a $π$-flux one. We also numerically realize three-dimensional pyrochlore and hyperhoneycomb lattices with space groups $R\bar{3}m$ and $C2/c$, the latter a nodal-loop semimetal with topological drumhead surface states. These results establish the BgV as a designable vacuum for all-dielectric tight-binding photonics in two and three dimensions.

Abstract: A new approach of silicon-lithium niobate (Si-LN) grating couplers (GCs) with a fully-etched structure is proposed. Abstract: Tapered optical fibers offer tunable dispersion and a small mode area, yielding high nonlinearity, which makes them ideal for nonlinear and quantum optics. Here, we show that when seeding with a broad spectrum, stimulated four-wave mixing yields a comb-like spectrum that is directly linked to the overall taper profile opening possibility for non-destructive evaluation of sub-micron taper. Our model based on joint-spectral amplitude includes the phase accumulated over the fiber length.

In terms of atomic Bose-Einstein condensates, these solutions represent matter-wave solitons with embedded vorticity. We examine the dependence of the solution families on the propagation constant, the depth and width of the guiding profile, and $\ell $. It is found that the increase of $\ell $ requires one to use a stronger or wider waveguiding potential to sustain localized states satisfying the Vakhitov-Kolokolov (VK) stability criterion. VK-complying and non-complying branches of vortex-soliton families are separated by a degenerate Townes branch. Direct simulations reveal that, although the guiding profile substantially suppresses the collapse and extends the propagation distance of vortex bullets, their robustness is ultimately limited by azimuthal instabilities, whose growth is enhanced for values of $\ell $. These results demonstrate that engineered refractive-index landscapes significantly enlarge the stability domain of spatiotemporal vortex bullets and provide guidelines for supporting optical bullets carrying large orbital angular momenta.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测任务中183 GHz与229 GHz谱区的辐射测量需要低相噪、高频率稳定度且满足尺寸、重量、功率（SWaP）约束的本振。传统介质谐振振荡器加级联倍频方案需为不同频段配置专用链路，硬件复杂度高，且倍频会恶化相位噪声。

Result: 数值仿真表明，同一基础架构可经电滤波器调谐与梳阶选择覆盖183 GHz和229 GHz等多个本振频率，避免了专用于各频段的倍频链，并因未使用频率倍进而避免相位噪声恶化。

Conclusion: 可重构自振荡微波光子架构为多频段星载辐射计本振提供了一种简化硬件、保持低相噪的可行方案，但当前结论基于数值仿真，仍需实验验证其相位噪声、频率稳定度与SWaP表现。

Abstract: Earth observation missions rely on microwave and sub-terahertz radiometry for retrieving atmospheric parameters such as temperature, humidity, and cloud properties, with the 183- and 229-GHz spectral regions being particularly relevant. Local-oscillator (LO) generation at these frequencies requires low phase noise and high frequency stability while meeting stringent size, weight, and power (SWaP) constraints. Conventional architectures employ dielectric resonator oscillators followed by cascaded frequency-multiplication stages, requiring dedicated generation chains for different frequency bands and increasing hardware complexity. Furthermore, frequency multiplication degrades the phase noise of the generated signal. This work proposes and numerically investigates a reconfigurable self-oscillating microwave photonic architecture for LO generation in Earth observation receivers. Cascaded Mach-Zehnder and phase modulation within an optoelectronic feedback loop generate an optical frequency comb with enhanced higher-order components. A combination of optical filters is used to select the required comb line. First, a conventional bandpass filter with a bandwidth of approximately 10 GHz isolates the spectral region containing the desired component, after which a narrowband dual cavity Bragg grating ring resonator (BGRR) with a bandwidth of approximately 3 MHz provides the final line selection. The selected comb line is then combined with an optical reference derived from the same laser and photodetected to generate the desired millimeter-wave or sub-terahertz signal through optical heterodyning. Numerical simulations are presented for 183- and 229-GHz generation, demonstrating the ability of the same underlying architecture to address multiple LO frequencies by tuning the OEO electrical filter to the appropriate oscillation frequency and selecting the corresponding comb order.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 电子参数化+可编程光子网格可解耦参数结构与光路设计，兼顾压缩效率与表达能力，为参数高效神经网络计算提供可行路径。

Abstract: Memory capacity and bandwidth are becoming major constraints in neural-network computing, motivating parameter-efficient representations that reduce model storage and data movement. Tensor-network methods already enable substantial compression of neural networks, but their structured operations are often poorly matched to conventional processors optimized for dense linear algebra. Such representations can be implemented in photonic hardware, although this typically requires optical architectures tailored to a particular factorization. Here we propose a different approach based on programmable photonic meshes. Compact electronic parameters determine the interferometer phases, while optical interference produces a dense, generally high-rank transformation. Since the parameterization remains electronic, its dimensionality and structure can be changed without modifying the optical circuit. Numerical experiments in end-to-end training demonstrate that the proposed photonic layers match or outperform strongly compressed tensor-train and brickwork layers, often with fewer trainable parameters. Experiments in parameter-efficient fine-tuning of a language model also show advantages over established digital adapters. The reduced trainable state lowers persistent memory requirements and offers the potential to reduce data movement and bandwidth demands.

</details>

Abstract: We describe a class of electromagnetic Gaussian modes with spherical spatiotemporal structure that remains invariant during propagation through anomalously dispersive media. As a result, the modes support spatiotemporal orbital angular momentum (OAM) configurations with longitudinal and transverse components set by the orientation of the quantization axis. Superpositions of these modes generalize the familiar solution of a chirped pulse that simultaneously focuses and compresses to arbitrary angular structure. The modes are derived through a series of unitary transformations that map the dispersive paraxial wave equation onto a Schrödinger equation with a 3D isotropic harmonic potential. These transformations facilitate the identification of conserved operators for mixed longitudinal and transverse OAM.

Abstract: In conventional coupled oscillator lattices and laser arrays, expanding the active volume inevitably causes spectral crowding and degrades spatial uniformity, posing a long-standing barrier to high-power single-mode operation. We uncover a dissipative selection mechanism in sparse star networks that decouples modal selectivity from lattice size constraints. By engineering dissipative auxiliary couplings between a central hub and N peripheral resonators, the system establishes an exact star-Laplacian symmetry. This enforces a target eigenstate that is immune to all lossy channels while maintaining equal amplitude across all main nodes. All competing eigenstates break this symmetry, driving the auxiliary loss and opening a persistent, size-independent modal loss gap that prevents multi-mode competition. Realistic microring simulations validate the robustness of this dark-state isolation under electromagnetic dispersion and mechanism extension to arbitrary arrays is allowed when meets the system's exceptional point. By exploiting graph topology to bypass multi-mode competition, this work establishes a general route toward scalable high-power single-supermode laser arrays and robust state selection in coupled non-Hermitian systems.

Abstract: Locally recoverable codes (LRCs) permit reconstruction of a lost coordinate from a small subset of the remaining coordinates, a property that is a fundamental coding technique for distributed storage and, more recently, for fault-tolerant quantum memories and distributed storage systems. Existing constructions of quantum locally recoverable codes (qLRCs) and their entanglement-assisted generalizations (EA-qLRCs) impose a single locality parameter $r$ governing recovery from both bit-flip ($X$-type) and phase-flip ($Z$-type) errors, even though physical noise is rarely symmetric between the two. We introduce entanglement-assisted quantum locally recoverable codes (EA-qLRC) with \emph{asymmetric locality} $\{r_X,r_Z\}$, in which $X$-type and $Z$-type errors are corrected from recovery sets of size at most $r_X+1$ and $r_Z+1$, respectively. We extend a stabilizer sufficient condition for this property and, in particular, the CSS framework, where asymmetric locality corresponds to separate locality conditions on the two classical codes $\mathcal C_1$ and $\mathcal C_2$ underlying the construction. From this characterization we derive Singleton-, Griesmer-, Plotkin-, sphere-packing-, and Cadambe\textendash Mazumdar-like bounds for CSS-type asymmetric EA-qLRCs (AEA-qLRCs) built from Proposition~\ref{CSS-EA-qLRC-Asym}, and we characterize exactly when a pure CSS-type AEA-qLRC built from a pair of classical LRCs attains the Singleton-like bound with equality.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子控制理论只能判定给定控制通道下量子门能否实现，却无法刻画其对误差的鲁棒性（resilience to errors）。本文旨在弥补这一空白，回答"何时存在稳健门实现"以及"如何构造稳健门"两个问题。

Method: 在$d$能级系统中建立稳健门实现的存在性判据：证明"所有门可实现的普适性"并不充分，普适稳健门族存在当且仅当受驱哈密顿量之间不存在公共对称性（no common symmetry）。在判据成立时，构造可同时抵御静态微扰、哈密顿量均匀重标定、有限选定频率处微扰以及任意选定波形微扰的门方案；并利用仅有的误差信息（如仅知噪音频峰频率）施加稳健条件，最后通过数值优化构造显式协议。

Result: 数值构造的显式稳健门协议在哈密顿量微扰与环境诱导噪声下，平均门保真度损失（average gate infidelity）降低约2至7个数量级；稳健条件可在误差信息有限时广泛施加，也可按平台定制（如针对主导谱峰的环境噪声，无需谱细节）。

Conclusion: 存在性判据与数值构造共同为评估量子平台潜力、设计稳健量子门提供了实用指导：可实现的普适性须辅以"无公共对称性"条件才能保证稳健性，且稳健门可按误差类型灵活定制。

Abstract: For given control channels, quantum control theory determines whether quantum gates can be realized, but not their resilience to errors. We address this problem by establishing existence criteria for robust gate implementation in $d$-level systems. Our criteria reveal that implementability of all gates is not enough: a universal family of robust gates exists if and only if the driven Hamiltonians share no common symmetry. When these criteria hold, we can make gates resilient to any combination of static perturbations, uniform Hamiltonian rescaling, perturbations at a finite set of selected frequencies, and perturbations with arbitrary selected waveforms. We can impose robustness conditions broadly with limited information about errors, e.g., against any static perturbations; we can also tailor these conditions to the platform, e.g., against environment-induced noise with dominant spectral peaks, requiring no spectral details beyond the peak frequencies. We numerically construct explicit protocols for robust gates, reducing average gate infidelity by approximately two to seven orders of magnitude under Hamiltonian perturbations and environment-induced noise. Together, our existence criteria and numerical constructions provide practical guidance for evaluating the potential of quantum platforms and designing robust quantum gates.

</details>

Abstract: Georgiev's analysis of a freely precessing spin correctly emphasizes that the Mandelstam-Tamm quantity $ΔT=Δ\hat A/|d\langle\hat A\rangle/d t|$ is not the variance of a time operator. He concludes, however, that its divergence near the extrema of the clock signal is devoid of physical meaning and that $ΔT$ does not disclose how well the system measures the passage of time. Its divergence at submaximal-coherence extrema therefore identifies a loss of local sensitivity in the selected readout, not an infinite variance of physical time; a complementary $\hatσ_y$ measurement remains informative. This interpretation also separates Georgiev's exactly known tick--tock period from the statistical precision with which a tick can be located. Abstract: Quantum error correction enables scalable fault-tolerant quantum computation by suppressing logical errors exponentially with code distance once physical error rates fall below a critical threshold. Here we demonstrate below-threshold operation of triangular color codes with distances $d=3,5,$ and $7$ on Quantinuum's Helios trapped-ion quantum processor. Leveraging both circuit- and hardware- level leakage reduction schemes, we achieve logical error rates as low as $5_{-2}^{+4}\times 10^{-6}$, and demonstrate error suppression by over an order of magnitude, $Λ=12_{-2}^{+3}$, with each successive increase in code distance. Moreover, we demonstrate below threshold scaling of error rates for Clifford two-qubit randomized benchmarking circuits when encoded in $d=3,5$ color codes, achieving logical error rate of $7^{+3}_{-3}\times 10^{-5}$ per logical two-qubit Clifford at distance $5$ with a suppression factor of $Λ=9_{-3}^{+8}$. We compare logical performance for existing near-optimal algorithmic decoders, and both convolutional neural network and transformer-based neural network decoders capable of real-time decoding an order of magnitude faster than the syndrome extraction rate. These results approach the logical error rates required for megaquop-scale quantum computation and indicate that scaled trapped-ion processors could reach application-relevant performance with substantially smaller code blocks than indicated by previous results in other qubit modalities.

Abstract: Quantum repeaters will be paramount to the success of the future quantum internet. Presently however, the losses incurred in fibre communications are unsustainable when fragile quantum states are transmitted over long distances. The most effective repeater nodes will require incorporation of a minimum of two quantum memory units (QMems) for successful operation. Therefore a network composed of numerous nodes will ideally require scalable repeaters that contain QMems that are portable, robust and do not require a high amount of overhead. Here we investigate the performance of a commercial QMem based on a warm vapour of Rb atoms. Specifically, we characterize the coherence time, absolute efficiency, signal to noise ratio and bit error ratio of this device over a range of vapour temperatures that are readily controllable. For our system, we place these performance metrics into the same conventions as those established for current optical communication protocols. Development of QMem systems meeting these benchmarks will be a vital step for seamless integration of QMems systems into present-day communication architectures.

Abstract: We construct translation-invariant pure quasifree fermionic states on the one-dimensional lattice whose half-chain von Neumann algebras are hyperfinite factors of type $\mathrm{II}_\infty$ or $\mathrm{III}_λ$ for any $0<λ\leq 1$. Via the Jordan-Wigner-Araki transformation, these yield translation-invariant pure spin-chain states with the same half-chain factors, demonstrating that such factors need not be of type I or III. Each state is the unique ground state of a gapless, translation-invariant parent Hamiltonian with superpolynomially decaying interactions. For any fixed factor type, the interval entanglement entropy can exhibit widely varying growth rates, from arbitrarily slow divergence to nearly power-law growth, showing that entropy scaling does not determine the factor type. In contrast, we prove that gauge-invariant quadratic Hamiltonians with finitely many flavours and exponentially decaying hopping exhibit a dichotomy: their ground states either satisfy an area law with a type I half-chain factor, or have logarithmic entropy growth with a type $\mathrm{III}_1$ factor. Abstract: We introduce Trajectree, a quantum trajectory-based quantum circuit simulator that uses a novel, state-saving partial execution of the quantum circuits to simulate broad and deep noisy quantum circuits efficiently. Using this architecture, we show that Trajectree can simulate standard benchmark noisy circuits over five times faster than Google's QSim, a part of the Cirq package. We also analyze Trajectree's performance for simulating quantum optical systems relevant to communication and computing applications. We demonstrate applications of the optical backend to simulate three optical experiments: CHSH Bell test, entanglement swapping, and optical CNOT gate. This shows Trajectree's potential for simulating noisy qudit and optical systems efficiently.

Abstract: The primary pathway to the investigation of exceptional points (EPs) in open quantum systems has been the use of Markovian master equations. However, recently it has been realised that they can arise in settings that show non-Markovian behaviour. The main approach in this direction has been the use of pseudomodes, which allow the influence of a structured environment to be replaced by new damped auxiliary modes. In this work, we use a Heisenberg-equation approach which relies on the reaction-coordinate formalism to obtain exact analytical dynamics. First, we show how our method considerably simplifies the analysis compared to the use of master equations. Second, we utilise it to discuss non-Markovian revivals in systems with second and higher-order EPs, showing that the notion that EPs demarcate the onset of revivals is not generally true. We then utilise our results to discuss the dynamical effects of higher-order non-Markovian EPs, deriving bounds on the number of revivals and investigating enhanced asymptotic relaxation near higher-order EPs. Finally, we investigate non-Markovian EPs with engineered reservoirs and show how they can be utilised to control the rate of irreversible information loss.

Abstract: Laser amplification kicks off with just a few photons in the cavity, a regime where standard additive Langevin noise cannot capture the real fluctuations. To understand how this amplification begins, we map out a single-mode photon-carrier model that links macroscopic lasers directly into nanolasers. Stationary rate equations reveal that stimulated emission takes over across a tight pump window. In nanolasers, however, spontaneous processes coexist across a much broader range. Because steady-state averages miss the individual events that lead to the macroscopic photon number, we frame the stochastic dynamics around the actual discrete transitions, deriving a state-dependent diffusion approximation. The destruction of an excited carrier concurrent with the generation of a photon creates a negative photon-carrier cross-correlation. This unavoidably leads to a correlated, multiplicative Gaussian-noise framework. We track the resulting discrete dynamics with an established, event-based Laser Markov Chain (LMC) which stochastically samples pump, relaxation, emission, and cavity-loss events keeping all physical quantities as non-negative integers. Comparing its predictions to those of our multiplicative model proves that the latter reproduces excellently well the statistical behavior of photons and carrier, as long as a continuous diffusion description holds. Once the emission drops into the few-photon regime, however, the discrete model becomes indispensable. On the other hand, at large photon counts relative fluctuations shrink to a level where the state-dependence of the diffusion matrix can be ignored, to leading order, thus returning to the classic Langevin description of the system's stochastic dynamics.

Abstract: Collective coupling to the Minkowski vacuum allows uniformly accelerated quantum detectors to exhibit a strong quantum Mpemba effect, in which a state initially farther from equilibrium relaxes faster than a closer one. We establish the existence of this effect for every fixed finite number of detectors $N\geq 2$ at sufficiently small transverse separation within the pointlike secular master equation. The mechanism originates from two reflection sectors whose populations are conserved by the leading collective dynamics. Weak intersector transfer at finite separation generates an isolated, parametrically slow relaxation mode. A simple family of initial states permits its leading-order cancellation by matching the equilibrium sector populations, while a corrected preparation eliminates the slow-mode amplitude exactly. We construct a reference state that establishes a genuine reversal of trace-distance ordering. Numerical calculations and local-rate-preserving controls identify the collective origin of the effect, while spatially smeared detector calculations support its persistence within the second-order secular framework. The same mechanism extends to detectors coupled to a common thermal field, with analytically distinct slow relaxation rates. Thus, strong Mpemba relaxation is not exclusive to acceleration but emerges from a more general interplay between collective dissipation, reflection-sector conservation, and weak symmetry-breaking transfer.

Abstract: The quantum phase of a multiparticle system has remained a long-standing mystery in quantum physics. The absence of a phase operator that faithfully represents this physical quantity has prevented the formulation of a meaningful uncertainty relation with particle number. Here, we resolve this problem by measuring, for the first time, the quantum phase of multiparticle systems containing up to fifteen particles. We achieve this through a new quantum state tomography scheme that combines homodyne interferometry with photon-number-resolving detection. This approach enables us to reconstruct complementary photon-number and phase representations and establish the corresponding number-phase uncertainty relation. Our measurements further provide access to the mutually unbiased basis of a multiparticle system, a fundamental resource for multiparticle quantum information processing. Furthermore, we report the spatially resolved characterization of the quantum phase. These results provide an operational framework for measuring quantum phase in multiparticle systems, with implications for fundamental quantum physics and scalable multiparticle quantum technologies.

Abstract: Mixed-state quantum geometry plays a fundamental role in quantum metrology, multiparameter estimation, and the characterization of quantum many-body systems. Here we address the challenge and develop a novel quantum algorithmic framework for efficiently accessing the quantum Fisher information matrix (QFIM) and quantum geometric tensor (QGT) of mixed states. A rigorous complexity analysis further establishes the resource efficiency of our algorithm, demonstrating logarithmic precision dependence and double-logarithmic ancillary-qubit overhead at fixed conditioning. Building on this construction, we achieve near-Heisenberg precision scaling for diagonal QFI estimation without quantum phase estimation and construct unified block encodings of the full QFIM and QGT under a bounded spectral-gap assumption. A complementary regularized algorithm extends efficient diagonal estimation to exponentially small spectral gaps under a bounded-response condition. Our results establish a resource-efficient route to coherent mixed-state quantum geometry, enabling its direct integration into quantum linear-algebra algorithms without classical reconstruction and opening new possibilities for quantum-enhanced multiparameter metrology.

Abstract: We study an adiabatic controlled-$Z$ (CZ) gate for neutral atoms in which dipole-dipole coupling between nearly degenerate Rydberg pair states enhances the interaction between dressed ground states. This coupling produces an avoided crossing in the two-atom dressed-state spectrum. Approaching the crossing enhances the dressed interaction, but also reduces the dressed-state gap and increases the Rydberg admixture. We identify a Förster-enhanced operating range in which the required conditional phase is accumulated with adiabatic return and small transient Rydberg population. We then analyze the finite-time dynamics of the two-qubit computational states under a smooth pulse. Reducing the dressing strength weakens the dressed interaction, so maintaining the target phase at the same pulse duration requires operation closer to the avoided crossing, where the Rydberg admixture increases. This dependence is then used to select the shortest gate at a fixed peak Rabi frequency in the Förster-enhanced operating range that accumulates the CZ phase with high Bell-state fidelity while keeping the Rydberg population small. Changing the Förster defect shifts the avoided crossing, while the gate parameters change only weakly. For a specific rubidium Förster channel, the selected gate corresponds to experimentally relevant laser coupling, pulse duration, and interatomic separation. We also estimate errors from the finite Rydberg lifetime and from weak excitation of a neighboring spectator atom. For the parameters considered, the spectator-induced error remains below the finite-lifetime contribution.

Abstract: We analytically characterize how the location and the local or correlated structure of stochastic Pauli errors affect a three-qubit coherent quantum-teleportation circuit. Independent local channels and a fully correlated two-qubit channel are inserted separately in six configurations. Propagating each Pauli-error realization through the Clifford circuit yields the effective channel on Bob's qubit and analytical expressions for the average and state-resolved fidelities. Under a common ordered-pair probability convention, the six configurations form three pairs that induce equivalent reduced channels on Bob. Within each pair, corresponding Pauli-error realizations have the same probabilities and produce the same effective Pauli operator on Bob's output state. The equivalence therefore holds for every input state within the stochastic Pauli models considered here, rather than only for the average fidelity. Correlated Z errors do not affect the output for the first pair; local X and Z errors exhibit complementary qubit sensitivity for the second; and correlated same-axis Pauli errors cancel for the third. These results classify the error response of the selected circuit configurations and show that it depends jointly on the error statistics, Pauli axis, and position within the protocol.

As a proof-of-concept for selective molecular-spin sensing, histidine addition extends T_2^* to ~0.8 ns in a concentration-dependent manner, whereas glycine produces no measurable effect. The resulting eigenstate remains embedded in the continuum of other sectors and carries an inverse-cube waveguide amplitude, with coherent contributions from direct plaquette--guide coupling and long-range guide propagation. Abstract: We characterize the hybrid Fourier transform on the Schwartz--Grassmann space $\caS(\bbR^m)\otimesΛ_{2n}$ by operator intertwining relations. This rigidity result is obtained by computing the ordinary commutants of the even and full quadratic oscillator actions of $\fosp(4n|2m)$. After a degree-scaling change of normalization, we make the hybrid Fourier transform unitary with respect to the occupation number inner product, and factor it into a bosonic oscillator rotation and (up to a scalar phase) an $SU(2)$ rotation in the fermionic Lefschetz representation. For $m=1$ and $n\ge2$, we show that full even quadratic covariance together with the parity-preserving cubic relation for $qQ_f$ determines the unitary transform up to a global phase and a fermion-parity phase. Abstract: Reliable quantum computation requires methods for verifying quantum circuits. Level-synchronized tree automata (LSTAs) were recently introduced to represent sets of quantum states for this purpose. We study LSTAs along two axes relevant to verification: the complexity of their decision problems and the size of their representations under circuit operations. We prove that acyclic LSTAs have NP-complete nonemptiness and $Π_2^P$-complete language inclusion, and that general language inclusion is EXPSPACE-complete. We further prove inherent quadratic representation growth under individual quantum gates in a fixed qubit order, and give an adjacent-qubit reordering construction that preserves level synchronization. These results clarify the scope of applicability of the automata-theoretic toolbox for quantum circuit verification.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Inner products of random matrices and random quantum states---such as orbital inner products, expectation values, overlaps, and cyclic Bargmann invariants---are shown to follow from two underlying mechanisms: the diagonal of an induced random state is Dirichlet distributed, and the expectation value of a random pure state in a rank-one or two-by-two operator is a numerical shadow on an elliptical disk. For states induced by an environment of dimension $K$, the expectation value of any observable is a Curry--Schoenberg spline with each eigenvalue repeated $K$ times, and the vector of moments has an explicit density on a simplex; for a qutrit, the variance law is obtained in finite form. The overlap of two random qubit states attains its mode at its mean for every rotation-invariant ensemble, but for Hilbert--Schmidt qutrits the density at the mean has a strictly negative derivative, so mode and mean separate; for large environments, the centred overlap converges to a variance-gamma law rather than a Gaussian. For two noncommuting qubit observables, the joint law of the two standard deviations is derived for all $K$, and the probability of coming within a small distance of the optimal sum of variances scales as a power of that distance, with a different exponent when the Bloch axes are orthogonal. For three Haar-random qubit states, the exact density of the Bargmann invariant is given in terms of a complete elliptic integral, together with its local behaviour and elementary marginals. In every dimension, the probability that its real part is negative---certifying basis-independent coherence---is computed, along with all mixed moments. For cycles of arbitrary length, the invariant is shown to be driven by an exact three-parameter Markov chain, and the logarithm of its modulus obeys a law of large numbers and a central limit theorem.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QKD在理想假设下可安全分发密钥，但实际系统仍面临针对光信道、探测器与控制器的实现层攻击；直接分析原始量子态代价高、不可行，因此需要基于可观测运行日志的取证分析手段。

Conclusion: QF-LOG在受控合成场景中可实现可解释的取证分类，但稳定的内部评估不能证明其在QKD运行条件变化下的可靠性；该框架为后续在更广泛模拟设置及硬件生成QKD日志上的评估提供了有据可依的基础。

Abstract: Quantum Key Distribution (QKD) supports secure key establishment under ideal protocol assumptions, but practical systems remain exposed to implementation-level attacks against optical channels, detectors, and controller behavior. This study presents Quantum Forensic Log (QF-LOG), a simulation-based forensic framework for analyzing aggregated QKD operational logs rather than raw quantum states. QF-LOG uses four observable features: quantum bit error rate (QBER), photon count, latency in milliseconds, and abort flag. The framework classifies normal operation and three simulated attack-related conditions: partial intercept-resend, detector-blind, and fiber-tap. A physics-inspired Generator-A dataset with 5 million balanced records was constructed using explicit mathematical generation models for all four features. A transparent threshold baseline, logistic regression, random forest, and XGBoost were evaluated on Generator-A. The full-feature learning models achieved macro F1-scores from 0.8211 to 0.8243 and outperformed the transparent two-feature comparator. Feature diagnostics showed that QBER contributed most, followed by latency in milliseconds and photon count, while no single feature reproduced the full feature set performance. Robustness was assessed on a separately generated Generator-B dataset of 5 million records that uses the same governing equations under shifted simulated operating parameters. Without retraining or tuning, their macro F1-scores decreased to values from 0.5645 to 0.5669. These findings show that QF-LOG can support interpretable forensic classification in a controlled synthetic setting, but stable internal evaluation does not establish reliability under changed simulated QKD operating conditions. The framework provides a documented basis for future evaluation across broader simulated settings and hardware-generated QKD logs.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算依赖高编码率且距离足够的量子纠错码；现有代数构造（如lifted product码）从经典基矩阵生成大规模qLDPC码，但基矩阵设计空间大、缺乏系统性搜索手段。

Conclusion: 生成式扩散采样可作为量子码构造的有效搜索范式，在LP码框架内实现参数与解码性能的显著提升。

Abstract: The realization of fault-tolerant quantum computation relies on Quantum Error-Correcting Codes (QECC). Of particular interest are codes that combine a high encoding rate with sufficient distance. Existing algebraic constructions, such as lifted product (LP) codes, build a large quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) code from a classical base matrix. In this work we introduce LiftDiff, a generative approach to the discovery of QECC. A discrete denoising diffusion model is trained to generate base matrices over the group algebra $\mathbb{F}_2[\mathbb{Z}_\ell]$, which are then expanded into LP codes. The reverse diffusion process is guided by an energy function that evaluates quantum code properties, including girth, degree, sparsity and decoding performance, steering the sampler toward matrices that yield LP codes with favorable parameters. Our method discovers codes with parameters such as $[[169,13,11]]$, $[[143,15,9]]$, and $[[260,24,10]]$, including a $[[247,19,14]]$ code that achieves the highest ${kd^2}/{n}$ among the baselines. Moreover, several discovered codes outperform the Gross code across a broad range of the tested physical error rates under both code-capacity and circuit-level noise.

</details>

*Di Fang,Jiaqi Zhang*

TL;DR: 本文提出 Sobolev–Galerkin 框架，量化无界哈密顿量量子模拟中的空间离散化误差，对多体库仑系统给出周期化与 Galerkin 误差估计，并首次给出空间误差完全受控的端到端复杂度界。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非相对论量子化学建立在连续无界空间上的薛定谔方程，经典与量子算法通常截断空间域并引入有限维基底，把连续问题化为有限维问题。关键问题是：这种近似在多大程度上捕捉了原始连续问题？此前缺乏对空间离散误差的系统、严格估计。

Method: 建立通用的 Sobolev–Galerkin 空间离散误差估计框架，应用于多体库仑系统：证明波函数动力学与基态能量的周期化（periodization）误差和 Galerkin 误差估计；动力学中除精确波函数的有限基投影误差外，还引入额外的动力学投影误差，并用内积论证（inner-product argument）加以控制；分析依赖具有系统尺寸显式多项式依赖的多体 Sobolev 估计；最后与先进量子算法结合。

Abstract: Nonrelativistic quantum chemistry is based on the Schrödinger equation in continuous, unbounded space. Computational approaches on classical and quantum computers often truncate the spatial domain and introduce a finite-dimensional basis, reducing the continuum problem to a finite-dimensional one. We apply this framework to many-body Coulomb systems and establish both periodization and Galerkin error estimates for wave-function dynamics and ground-state energies. For dynamics, the finite-basis projection error of the exact wave function does not capture the full basis-discretization error: an additional dynamical projection error enters our bound at a different rate, and we introduce an inner-product argument to control this contribution. Interestingly, the spatial convergence rates for wave-function dynamics and ground-state energies differ quantitatively. For ground-state energies, we establish a sharp $M^{-1}$ convergence rate at fixed cell size, where $M$ is the number of one-particle basis functions. We also show that a uniform $M^{-1}$ convergence rate is impossible for the corresponding real-time wave-function error with general $H^2$ initial data. The analysis relies on many-body Sobolev estimates with explicit polynomial dependence on system size. Combining these estimates with state-of-the-art quantum algorithms, we obtain, to our knowledge, the first rigorous end-to-end complexity bounds for quantum simulation of molecular and electronic dynamics with all spatial errors explicitly controlled. The number of basis functions is determined directly from the continuum problem, without regularization.

Abstract: We show positive and negative results on classical relay channels with entanglement assistance, revealing a sharp contrast between strictly causal and causal relay encoding. Abstract: Bosonic particles beyond the Standard Model, such as axions, are among the leading candidates for dark matter. Abstract: We propose a scalable and efficient \textit{in situ} method for assessing the multiparticle indistinguishability of identical bosons or fermions in quantum interference experiments on linear interferometers by harnessing quantum parallelism and the bunching/anti-bunching tendencies of these particles. Abstract: Two-band 2D fractional Chern insulators (FCIs) have recently been shown, in principle, to admit unusual anyonic order, associated with Bloch Hopfions localized in momentum space. Here we show that bounding the integrated quantum metric along one primitive Brillouin-zone cycle may refine this topological order to $|C|$ distinct braiding observables, cyclically permuted by lattice translations, for Chern numbers $|C| > 1$. The cyclic structure entails mildly non-abelian braiding phases and otherwise matches the spectral flow exhibited by the FCI's hybrid Wannier states, with the metric bound equivalently a bound on the states' real-space spread.
  We give a classification of quantum gates in principle implementable on this topological order; among them we find the non-Clifford T-gate and the non-Clifford entangling CCZ-gate. This points to novel pathways to fault-tolerant quantum processes within the known orbital structure of FCIs, potentially informing experimental searches towards hardware for future topological quantum computers.

</details>

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 


<details>

Motivation: 

Method: 

Result: 

Conclusion: 

Abstract: Randomness certification aims to guarantee that the output of an untrusted quantum device contains genuine randomness that remains unpredictable even given quantum side information held by an adversary. For a classical verifier interacting with a single quantum device, such a guarantee cannot rely on the physical separation underlying standard Bell tests and must instead follow from computational restrictions on the prover. Building on the framework of Computational Bell Inequalities, we distinguish classical and quantum virtual-input hiding, showing that the former suffices for proofs of quantumness, whereas the latter enables randomness certification against quantum side information.
  Our main technical contribution is to establish quantum virtual-input hiding for a commitment-based protocol derived from the proof of quantumness from one-way-functions by Morimae and Yamakawa. We show that statistical hiding together with ordinary computational binding against quantum polynomial-time senders suffices, without requiring stronger quantum binding notions such as sum-binding or collapse-binding. This yields a randomness-certification protocol from post-quantum statistically hiding bit commitments. In particular, existing constructions of such commitments from post-quantum one-way permutations imply that post-quantum one-way permutations suffice for randomness certification.
  We further instantiate our construction using quantum-secure trapdoor claw-free functions, obtaining an efficient classical verifier, and in the quantum random oracle model, obtaining randomness certification without additional computational assumptions but with an inefficient verifier. These instantiations separate the cryptographic properties sufficient for randomness certification from the additional structure required for efficient verification within our commitment-based framework.

</details>


### [66] [Quantum State Search with Classical Interactive Advice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25657)
*Mohammed Barhoush*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文证明：对Haar随机n量子比特目标态，验证者通过反射预言机查询q次并从无限算力的证明者接收B比特经典通信，要达到常数平均保真度需满足(q+1)²(B+1)=Ω(2ⁿ)。由此得到UniqueQMA与多项式消息QCIP的量子预言机分离，并构造出QMA-QCIP[2] gap问题的预言机，用于导出witness encryption与量子不可区分混淆的否定性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Aaronson–Kuperberg 的下界仅适用于"单条经典证明"情形，而在一般经典交互协议（验证者可发送大量消息、证明者算力不受限）下，验证者能否学到隐藏量子态并不清楚。作者希望把该下界从单次经典证明推广到任意经典交互，并据此刻画UniqueQMA与QCIP的能力差异，进而为密码学中的负面结论提供基础。

Method: 采用预言机（oracle）下的查询复杂度下界技术：验证者持有一个关于目标态的反射预言机，进行q次查询，期间与全能力证明者交换总计B比特的经典消息，交互可任意长、查询间计算不受限。对Haar随机n量子比特目标态，作者证明达到常数平均保真度所需的查询与通信满足乘积型下界(q+1)²(B+1)=Ω(2ⁿ)，本质上是把Aaronson–Kuperberg的单证明下界扩展到任意经典交互协议；再基于该下界构造酉预言机，得到UniqueQMA与QCIP的分离，以及QMA-QCIP[2] gap问题的预言机构造。

Result: 主要结果：(1) 交互式情形下的紧致下界(q+1)²(B+1)=Ω(2ⁿ)，覆盖任意经典交互与查询间无限计算；(2) 存在酉预言机 U，使得某语言 L∈UniqueQMA^U 但 L∉QCIP^U（多项式条经典消息），从而给出UniqueQMA与QCIP的量子预言机分离；(3) 给出QMA-QCIP[2] gap问题的预言机构造，在可经典访问QCIP[2]判定预言机的条件下，得到某QMA语言的平均情形困难性。

Conclusion: 该工作表明：即使证明者算力无限且可与其进行任意经典交互，验证者也难以借助反射查询获取隐藏量子态，经典通信的整体下界由查询数与通信量的乘积结构(q+1)²(B+1)刻画。由此确立了UniqueQMA相对QCIP在预言机下的严格优势，并为QMA-QCIP[2] gap问题提供条件性构造，作为导出witness encryption与量子不可区分混淆之否定性结果的工具。

Abstract: We study whether a quantum verifier can find a hidden quantum state by exchanging classical messages with an unbounded prover who knows the state. The verifier has access to a reflection oracle about the target. For a Haar-random $n$-qubit target, we prove that achieving constant average fidelity with the target requires $(q+1)^2(B+1)=Ω(2^n)$, where $q$ is the number of verifier queries and $B$ is the total prover-to-verifier communication. The bound allows arbitrary classical interaction and unrestricted computation between queries. Our proof extends the lower bound of Aaronson and Kuperberg from a single classical proof to an arbitrary classical interaction.
  As a result, we obtain a quantum-oracle separation of UniqueQMA from QCIP with polynomially many classical messages. In particular, we give a unitary oracle $\mathcal{U}$ such that there exists a language $L\in\mathrm{UniqueQMA}^{\mathcal{U}}$ but $L\notin\mathrm{QCIP}^{\mathcal{U}}$.
  We further give an oracle construction for a notion known as QMA-QCIP[2] gap problem. This gives average case hardness for a QMA language under classical access to a QCIP[2] decision oracle and is used in establishing negative results on witness encryption and quantum indistinguishability obfuscation.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一全局相位标签不是保局域性的程序变换，因为所有参与方都必须作用于同一辅助系统；因此需要为每个参与方配置局域相位参考，以便在实Hilbert空间中局域地模拟多体复数幅度动力学。

Method: 为n方引入n个局域相位标签及显式码字|0⟩_{P_n}、|1⟩_{P_n}，证明单标签上的J=XZ实现乘i的逻辑作用；将局域复门A=A_R+iA_I编译为Â_j=A_R⊗I+A_I⊗J_j，并构造面向black-and-white misty门片段的中间语言。

Result: 证明任意有限局域纯态电路的intertwining定理、计算基测量概率的精确保持、运行期交互图保持及每方一rebit开销；对三体GHZ程序，翻译完全落在misty门片段内，以controlled-J替代复相位操作，借助不变量J_jJ_k|0⟩_P=−|0⟩_P传递分支符号，给出四个GHZ输入的正确性短证明并解释先前64路径相消计算。

Conclusion: 区分了“程序实化”与“寻找同关联的任意实策略”这两类问题，并明确界定哪些部分属于已知实Hilbert空间模拟、哪些属于本文发展的misty语言编译层。

Abstract: Complex amplitudes can be simulated over real Hilbert spaces by adjoining a two-dimensional real/imaginary degree of freedom. In a multipartite program, however, one globally shared phase tag is not a locality-preserving program transformation: every party would have to act on the same auxiliary system. We recast the distributed real simulation of multipartite quantum mechanics as a syntax-directed compiler into a real misty-state intermediate language. For $n$ parties we give explicit phase-reference codewords $|0\rangle_{P_n}$ and $|1\rangle_{P_n}$ on $n$ local tags and prove that the same real operator $J=XZ$ on any single tag realizes the logical action of multiplication by $i$. If a complex party-local gate is $A=A_R+iA_I$, its real translation is \[
  \widehat A_j=A_R\otimes I+A_I\otimes J_j, \] which acts only on party $j$'s data and local phase tag. We prove an intertwining theorem for arbitrary finite party-local pure-state circuits, exact preservation of computational-basis measurement probabilities, preservation of the run-time interaction graph, and one-rebit-per-party overhead. For the canonical three-party GHZ program, the translation lies entirely inside the ordinary black-and-white misty gate fragment: controlled-$J$ replaces the complex phase operation, while the invariant $J_jJ_k|0\rangle_P=-|0\rangle_P$ transfers the required branch sign. This yields a short correctness proof for all four promised GHZ inputs and explains the earlier 64-path cancellation calculation at a single logical phase-code boundary. We distinguish this program realification problem from the weaker task of finding any real strategy with the same correlations, and state precisely which parts are known real-Hilbert-space simulation and which parts belong to the misty-language compilation layer developed here.

</details>


### [68] [Perturbation theory for quantum information quantities of Gaussian states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25792)
*Zihao Gong,Saikat Guha*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多量子信息任务受益于高斯量子资源，其性能极限由量子信息量刻画，但此前缺乏直接在高斯态形式体系内处理这些信息量的统一微扰理论。

Method: 利用参考高斯态矩的扰动，对冯·诺依曼熵、量子相对熵、量子Chernoff量与量子保真度建立微扰理论；借助一般Fréchet导数恒等式，针对辛本征值均大于1/2的协方差矩阵推导紧凑的二阶表达式；证明微扰量子Chernoff量在其对称点取极大从而给出量子Chernoff指数；分析辛本征值等于1/2时导致的较慢渐近消失；对同时含等于与大于1/2辛本征值的协方差矩阵给出充分条件，使前者决定主导贡献且其余模可被追迹消除而不改变主导阶渐近标度或系数。

Result: 得到四个量子信息量的紧凑二阶微扰表达式；证明微扰量子Chernoff量在对称点取极大并由此得到量子Chernoff指数；揭示辛本征值等于1/2会引起更慢的渐近消失，可能改善相应基木性能极限；在量子照明与非相干光亚瑞利目标分辨中，直接在高斯态形式体系内复现了已知的渐近量子Chernoff指数，且较慢的渐近消失解释了后者中量子Chernoff指数随源间距的已知二次标度。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一高斯态微扰理论为量子信息量的二阶分析提供了简洁工具，统一并复现了量子照明与亚瑞利分辨中的已知渐近结果，并阐明辛本征值等于1/2处的退化是理解性能极限改善及二次标度现象的关键。

Abstract: Many quantum-information tasks can benefit from Gaussian quantum resources, while their fundamental performance limits are characterized by quantum information quantities. However, a unified perturbation theory for these quantities directly within the Gaussian-state formalism has been lacking. We develop such a theory for the von Neumann entropy, quantum relative entropy, quantum Chernoff quantity, and quantum fidelity, using perturbations of the moments of a reference Gaussian state. Using general Fréchet-derivative identities, we derive compact second-order expressions for reference-state covariance matrices whose symplectic eigenvalues are all greater than $1/2$. We also show that the perturbative quantum Chernoff quantity is maximized at its symmetric point, yielding the quantum Chernoff exponent (QCE). We further show that symplectic eigenvalues equal to 1/2 can lead to slower asymptotic vanishing, potentially improving the corresponding fundamental performance limits. For reference-state covariance matrices containing symplectic eigenvalues both equal to and greater than 1/2, we identify sufficient conditions under which the former determine the leading covariance-matrix contributions, allowing the remaining modes to be traced out without changing the leading-order asymptotic scaling or the coefficient. Applying the framework to quantum illumination and incoherent-light sub-Rayleigh object discrimination, we recover known asymptotic QCEs directly within the Gaussian-state formalism. In the latter problem, the slower asymptotic vanishing explains the known quadratic scaling of the QCE with the source separation.

</details>

*Chenjia Zhu,Shuai Xu,Yousheng Zhang,Yunfeng Liang,Yue Yang*

TL;DR: 该综述系统梳理了量子计算在磁约束聚变等离子体模拟中的现有研究（动理学、波相互作用、平衡、宏观稳定性），评估其硬件实现与运行瓶颈，指出目前成果多局限于线性化方程、约化模型或孤立子程序，并给出可能实现量子优势的问题特征与路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁约束聚变等离子体模拟涉及高维动理学、强非线性耦合、复杂几何与多尺度时空行为，带来严重计算瓶颈；量子计算被视为潜在加速手段，但其对聚变研究的实际优势尚未得到证明，亟需系统评估现状与差距。

Method: 综述性分析：跨动理学动力学、波相互作用、平衡与宏观稳定性四类问题回顾既有量子计算研究，评估其硬件实现方式与运行瓶颈；归纳高维性、结构化动力学、测量需求等关键问题特征，据此识别最可能产生量子优势的应用场景。

Result: 现有研究普遍受限于线性化方程、约化模型或孤立计算子程序，与真实聚变模拟之间存在显著差距；明确了实现量子优势所需的问题特征条件。

Conclusion: 提出面向磁约束聚变等离子体模拟实际量子优势的潜在技术途径与路线图。

Abstract: Plasma simulations for magnetic-confinement fusion (MCF) involve high-dimensional kinetic dynamics, strong nonlinear coupling, complex geometries, and multiscale spatiotemporal behavior, imposing severe computational bottlenecks. While quantum computing offers a potential alternative for accelerating plasma simulation problems, its practical advantage for fusion research has not yet been demonstrated. We review existing quantum computing studies across kinetic dynamics, wave interactions, equilibrium, and macroscopic stability, evaluating their hardware implementations and operational bottlenecks. Current studies remain largely restricted to linearized equations, reduced models, or isolated computational subroutines, leaving a substantial gap from realistic fusion simulations. We summarize key problem characteristics, including high dimensionality, structured dynamics, and measurement requirements, under which plasma applications are most likely to yield a quantum advantage. Guided by these considerations, we highlight potential approaches and present a roadmap toward practical quantum advantage of simulating plasma in MCF.

</details>

*Soumyakanti Bose,Hyukjoon Kwon,Yong Siah Teo,Hyunseok Jeong,Luis L. Sánchez-Soto*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态支撑诸多反经典直觉的现象，随着量子技术加速发展，驾驭这些量子资源的能力变得关键；因此需要明确量子性的量化方式及其与经典对应物的判别特征。

Method: 综述性研究：从理论（如量子性度量、非经典性判据）与操作（可实验检验的认证方案）两个层面，系统梳理量子态刻画的框架。

Result: 建立一个清晰的量子态刻画框架，将量子性的理论度量与操作层面的认证证据相衔接，用以区分量子态与其经典对应物。

Conclusion: 量子性认证不只是理论练习，而是识别并在实际应用中实现量子优势的实践必需。

Abstract: Quantum states are ultimately responsible for numerous counterintuitive phenomena that defy classical intuition. As the development of quantum technologies accelerates, our ability to harness these quantum resources becomes essential. This review addresses a fundamental question. How do we quantify the quantumness of a state, and which specific traits distinguish it from its classical counterparts? We examine quantumness through both theoretical and operational lenses, providing a clear framework for state characterization. Ultimately, we demonstrate that certifying quantumness is not just a theoretical exercise. It is a practical necessity for identifying, and realizing quantum advantage in real-world applications.

</details>

*Noah Kaufmann,Anders Søndberg Sørensen*

TL;DR: 研究时变信号传感中纠缠的优势：在经典白噪声退相干下，纠缠可在不损失灵敏度的前提下将带宽提高N倍；当探测时间受信号带宽限制时，固定带宽内的平均估计不确定度相比可分离态降低√N倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态信号情形下，N探针Ramsey光谱的量子优势（源自探针间非经典关联带来的灵敏度提升）对退相干极其敏感，严重限制了纠缠可带来的增益，因此需要考察纠缠在其他传感场景中的作用。

Method: 考虑时变信号的传感，选取经典白噪声引起的退相干作为最简单的例子，分析在估计时变信号频率分量时纠缠态的表现，并与静态信号及可分离态的基准进行对比。

Result: 在经典白噪声退相干下，纠缠使带宽提升N倍且不牺牲灵敏度；当探测时间受信号带宽而非退相干限制时，固定带宽上的平均估计不确定度相比可分离态降低√N倍。

Conclusion: 纠缠在时变信号传感中可显著提升带宽或平均估计精度，规避了静态Ramsey光谱中纠缠增益受退相干严重制约的问题。

Abstract: The use of entanglement in sensing is often motivated by improved sensitivity arising from non-classical correlations among probes. For static signals, however, this quantum advantage in Ramsey spectroscopy with $N$ probes is known to be highly susceptible to dephasing, which severely limits the possible gain from entanglement. In contrast, we consider sensing of signals that vary in time. Considering the simplest example of dephasing by classical white noise, we show that entanglement allows a large increase in bandwidth when estimating the frequency components of a time-dependent signal. Without sacrificing any sensitivity, entanglement allows for an increase in bandwidth by a factor of $N$. Alternatively, when interrogation times are limited by the signal bandwidth rather than by dephasing, the average estimation uncertainty over a fixed bandwidth is reduced by a factor of $\sqrt{N}$ compared with separable states.

</details>


### [72] [Efficient Block Encodings for Ensembles of Molecular Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25968)
*Cristian L. Cortes,Andrew J. Jenkins,William Pol,Dario Rocca,Mark Steudtner,Gian-Luca R. Anselmetti,Matthias Degroote,Nikolaj Moll,Raffaele Santagati,Michael Streif,Markus R. Hermann*

TL;DR: 提出SVD-THC方法，将张量超收缩扩展至多几何构型哈密顿量系综，构造单一复用块编码电路，使Toffoli复杂度随构型数近乎不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确刻画分子性质与反应性需对构型系综进行电子结构计算；量子算法需兼顾先进因子化技术与对系综规模的亚线性缩放，现有单构型块编码切片拼接效率低。

Method: 对多核构型哈密顿量系综做奇异值分解，将张量超收缩（THC）扩展为SVD-THC，据此构造覆盖整个系综的单一复用（multiplexed）块编码电路。

Result: 在1-溴-2-氯乙烷及甘氨酸、丙氨酸二肽的内旋转坐标系综（最多5,184个构型）上评估；网格规模相差576倍时Toffoli复杂度变化不超过12%；在能量阈值判定电路中较逐构型块编码切片拼接减少35–200倍Toffoli门。

Conclusion: SVD-THC使构型系综的相干处理成为容错量子计算中的实用原语。

Abstract: Accurately characterizing molecular properties and reactivity often requires electronic-structure calculations across an ensemble of molecular geometries. Quantum algorithms that process such ensembles coherently could address this challenge, but they require Hamiltonian block encoding circuits that combine advanced factorization techniques with sublinear scaling in ensemble size. Here, we introduce SVD-THC, which extends tensor hypercontraction to sets of Hamiltonians defined at different nuclear geometries through a singular value decomposition of the ensemble. We use SVD-THC to construct a single multiplexed block encoding circuit for all geometries in an ensemble. We then evaluate the approach on ensembles generated by varying internal rotational coordinates in 1-bromo-2-chloroethane as well as in glycine and alanine dipeptides, corresponding to established model systems for peptide conformational landscapes, with up to 5,184 geometries. Across several resolutions of the internal-coordinate grids, the Toffoli complexity of the resulting circuits grows only weakly with the number of geometries, changing by at most 12\% over grids that differ in size by up to 576$\times$. In an energy-thresholding circuit, this yields 35--200$\times$ fewer Toffoli gates than slicing together single-geometry block encodings. This approach could help make coherent processing of molecular geometry ensembles a useful primitive for fault-tolerant quantum computing.

</details>


### [73] [Conjugate measurement-induced evolution towards maximally mixed states in quadratic quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25976)
*S. Adarsh,T. P. Sreeraj*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究单模子系统耦合两模环境的完整二次动力学，通过精确追踪约化Weyl特征函数与Wigner函数，揭示其在相空间任意紧致区域上丧失对局部坐标的依赖，从而解释了子系统趋于最大混合态的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期工作已发现，在相互作用主导情形下，将子系统的共轭变量分别耦合到不同环境模式会使子系统趋于最大混合态；本文旨在将结论推广到完整的二次动力学，并找出导致该渐近行为的相空间机制。

Method: 考虑单模子系统耦合两模环境，分析其完整二次哈密顿动力学；利用精确解追踪约化Weyl特征函数与约化Wigner函数随时间的演化，并施加有限体积与动量正则化。

Conclusion: 子系统趋于最大混合态的根源在于约化相空间分布函数在紧致区域上的"坐标平坦化"，临界耦合值的存在区分了自由粒子与谐振子两类模型的渐近行为。

In free-particle model, this happens for all values of coupling, whereas in the harmonic oscillator model, it happens above a critical value of the coupling. Under a finite-volume and momentum regularization, this behavior corresponds to the reduced subsystem approaching a maximally mixed state.

Abstract: Satellite-based quantum links are a promising enabler of long-distance entanglement distribution, overcoming the exponential losses of optical fibers. Single-satellite dual-uplink configurations are additionally limited by the requirement of simultaneous visibility between both ground stations and the satellite.

The proposed topology introduces an inter-satellite link (ISL) that decouples the ground-station separation from the atmospheric uplink geometry. A realistic channel model is employed, incorporating geometric attenuation, atmospheric turbulence, background noise, and Bell-state measurement efficiency. The overall success probability and practical fidelity are evaluated as functions of satellite altitude and ground-station separation.

Abstract: We present a fully quantum theory of time-resolved transient absorption spectroscopy, providing a general framework for describing the linear response of a nonequilibrium target to quantum probe fields beyond the semiclassical approximation. We elucidate how quantum correlations between probe-field frequency modes couple to coherences in the target and derive a simple expression for computing the resulting spectroscopic signals. We identify conditions under which quantum light can modify transient absorption spectra beyond the limits achievable with classical fields. Our work establishes a theoretical foundation for exploiting quantum light in ultrafast spectroscopy and suggests new routes for resolving dynamics in matter on timescales shorter than the duration of the probe pulse.

</details>

Abstract: Light curves reveal stellar variability and support studies of stellar evolution and cosmic distances. Reliable classification is therefore important for large time-domain surveys. We present HyQ-LC for ten-class stellar light-curve classification using 359 handcrafted features and 633,034 training objects. The main model combines a window multilayer perceptron (MLP) backbone with a nine-qubit readout using amplitude encoding and angle re-uploading. Both this hybrid and its pure-MLP control have 44,238 parameters. On a shared 20,000-object test set, the hybrid has macro-averaged F1 (Macro-F1) point gains of 0.8276 and 1.2393 percentage points over the pure MLP on two fine-tuning seeds. A separate fixed-fusion configuration achieves slightly higher Macro-F1 but lower accuracy than approximately budget-matched LightGBM. Its positive class-level differences are concentrated in CEP, RRC, and ROT, while the full-budget LightGBM reference scores higher on both metrics. All circuits are simulated classically. The comparisons retain existing validation-selected checkpoints and use the same test objects, with features, predictions, and recomputation code publicly available. The results identify point-estimate improvements over the tested controls while leaving the specific contribution of the quantum branch unresolved.

</details>

Abstract: Quantum communication networks promise information-theoretic security but this security collapses if intermediate relay nodes are compromised, a risk that grows sharply when compromised nodes cannot be identified. We address this twofold: first, we show that multi-path routing provides quantifiable protection against unidentified compromised nodes. Second, we provide a comprehensive statistical and graph-theoretic analysis of network and node security under compromise, covering full and partial node compromise, optimal placement of additional edges via an iterative method, and comparative hacking-probability analysis across network topologies. Together, these results identify the topology and multi-path routing strategy that most improves security for a given network. We apply this framework to quantum key distribution across multiple network topologies, demonstrating a scalable route to secure, reliable quantum communication under realistic, unidentified adversarial threats, without relying on quantum memory.

</details>

Result: 单Rabi电池中泵浦抑制相干能量回流、拓宽高能充电窗口，并提升对计时误差与失谐的鲁棒性，在选定工作点提高ergotropy及可提取能量占比；有限Dicke电池中泵浦同样稳定充电动力学，最强泵浦相对厄米参考提升峰值储能与单胞ergotropy及近峰值计时鲁棒性，平均充电功率亦增大（含泵浦辅助跃迁贡献）；等输入比较下共腔Dicke与独立Rabi电池的相对性能依赖泵浦输入上限。

Abstract: We investigate open-system charging protocols for Rabi and Dicke quantum batteries under direct incoherent pumping. The charging dynamics follows a Lindblad master equation, while effective non-Hermitian generators describe the conditioned no-jump mechanism. For a single Rabi battery, this pumping suppresses coherent energy backflow, broadens the high-energy charging window, and improves robustness against both timing errors and detuning. At the selected Rabi operating points, pumping also increases ergotropy and the extractable fraction of the stored energy. For finite Dicke batteries, direct incoherent pumping likewise stabilizes the charging dynamics. Across the finite-size scan, the strongest pump considered improves the peak stored energy and ergotropy per cell and near-peak timing robustness relative to the Hermitian reference. The maximum average charging power also increases, although this metric includes pump-assisted jump contributions. Equal-input comparisons show that the relative performance of common-cavity Dicke and independent Rabi batteries varies with the pump-input bound. Taken together, these results show that direct incoherent pumping provides a route to stabilized charging in Rabi and finite Dicke quantum batteries.

</details>


### [79] [Quantum control as renormalization group flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26154)
*Zuo Wang,Paul M. Schindler,Modesto Orozco-Ruiz,Nguyen H. Le,Florian Mintert,Marin Bukov*
