TL;DR: 该文建立量子控制的“重整化群”框架：在含时哈密顿量空间中构造流量，使目标幺正演化保持不变、同时单调削减控制子空间外的哈密顿量权重，并提出流方程工程设计方法，应用于非微扰Floquet工程与快速正向驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 哈密顿量RG在不同尺度间建立有效描述并诱导哈密顿量空间中的流；作者希望将这一思想移植到量子控制，以获得统一视角来组织看似不同的非平衡量子动力学问题（如Floquet工程与绝热捷径）。

Method: 在含时哈密顿量空间中构造流：以仅含实验可及项的规定控制子空间作为不动点流形；约束流保持目标幺正不变，并使子空间外哈密顿量权重单调下降。利用流构造的自由度提出“流方程工程”，支持时间周期协议、给定端点的开区间光滑协议等问题设定。

Result: 给出两类应用：(i) 超越高频展开适用域的非微扰Floquet工程，为Haldane模型构造可实现的时周期协议；(ii) 从单位保真度绝热反驱动（counterdiabatic）演化获得可实现的快速正向驱动，在大横向场Ising链、非绝热区域将长程多体绝热规范势压缩进原始含时Ising控制方案中。

Conclusion: 该控制RG框架为哈密顿量工程与幺正量子控制提供统一视角，可作为组织各类非平衡量子动力学的原则性框架。

Abstract: The Renormalization Group (RG) establishes relations between effective descriptions of physical systems at different scales; in Hamiltonian RG, these relations induce a flow in the space of Hamiltonians. In direct correspondence, we design an RG framework for quantum control by constructing a flow in the space of time-dependent Hamiltonians. A prescribed control subspace, selected to contain only experimentally accessible terms, forms the fixed-point manifold, while the flow preserves the target unitary and monotonically reduces the Hamiltonian weight outside this subspace. Exploiting the freedom in constructing the flow, we introduce flow-equation engineering to enhance the versatility of the framework for diverse control problems, including time-periodic and open-interval smooth protocols with prescribed endpoints. We provide two disparate applications to exhibit the utility of our approach: (i) Nonperturbative Floquet engineering beyond the regime of validity of high-frequency expansions, which we demonstrate by constructing an implementable time-periodic protocol for the Haldane model; (ii) Accessible fast-forward driving from unit-fidelity counterdiabatic evolution, which we showcase for large transverse-field Ising chains away from the adiabatic regime by compressing the long-range multibody adiabatic gauge potential into the original time-dependent Ising control schedules. Our control RG framework reveals a unifying perspective on Hamiltonian engineering and unitary quantum control, and can serve as an organizing principle behind seemingly disparate nonequilibrium quantum dynamics.

</details>


### [80] [A Readout-Aware Theory of Approximate Heisenberg Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26222)
*Yubraj Bhandari,Jakob S. Kottmann,Adelina Bärligea*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"广义泡利传播"统一框架，将泡利传播、李代数模拟与Krylov子空间方法纳入同一代数体系，并借助加权对易图给出近似误差的精确恒等式及"延迟可观测性"结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的海森堡图像期望值计算方法为保持可计算性，必须在演化中丢弃部分算符分量；然而这些分量的幅度本身并不能决定期望值误差，误差还取决于其后续演化与输入态。因此需要揭示丢弃分量如何影响目标期望值。

Method: 构建统一的代数框架，涵盖泡利传播、李代数模拟与Krylov子空间方法；引入加权对易图，推导将近似误差与所省略/丢弃算符分量的带符号贡献相联系的精确恒等式；在此基础上给出有限阶误差估计与依赖量子态的选择规则。

Result: 核心结论为"延迟可观测性"：给出被丢弃分量的贡献在传播时间低阶上消失的条件。进一步证明，在适当对称性条件下，保留弱扰动的对称保持部分可消除一阶期望值误差。

Conclusion: 该框架揭示了近似误差如何影响目标期望值，为更具信息量的误差分析以及"读出感知"的模拟策略设计提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Classical methods for computing quantum expectation values often propagate observables in the Heisenberg picture. To remain tractable, these methods rely on approximations that discard operator components during evolution. Yet the magnitude of these components alone does not determine the resulting expectation-value error, which also depends on their subsequent evolution and the input state. We develop \emph{generalized Pauli propagation}, a common algebraic framework encompassing Pauli propagation, Lie-algebraic simulation, and Krylov subspace methods. Using weighted commutator graphs, we derive exact identities that relate approximation errors to the signed contributions of omitted and discarded operator components. Our central result establishes \emph{delayed observability}, identifying conditions under which these contributions vanish at low orders in propagation time. We use this structure to develop finite-order error estimates and state-dependent selection rules. We further prove that retaining the symmetry-preserving part of a weak perturbation eliminates first-order expectation-value errors under suitable symmetry conditions. By revealing how approximation errors affect the desired expectation value, our framework provides a basis for more informative error analysis and the development of readout-aware simulation strategies.

</details>

Abstract: Sidewall morphology is a potentially important factor influencing superconducting qubit performance, yet its quantitative characterization remains challenging due to the absence of standardized measurement protocols. In this work, we develop a scanning probe microscopy-based measurement framework for the quantitative characterization of superconducting qubit sidewalls. The framework introduces a refined root mean square lateral sidewall roughness descriptor and a new curvature-equivalent radius for quantifying vertical sidewall morphology. The descriptors and measurement procedures are applied to different components of qubit devices, forming the basis of a proposed sidewall characterization protocol. This provides a foundation for quantitative comparisons of fabrication processes across laboratories, enabling future investigations of correlations between sidewall geometry and qubit performance.

</details>


### [82] [The Fundamental Memory Ceiling of Noisy Quantum Reservoirs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26228)
*Markus Baumann,Jonas Stein,Claudia Linnhoff-Popien*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在去极化噪声下，量子储备池的最长记忆时间由每步重新初始化的新鲜量子比特数 k 决定，其上界为 exp[Θ(k)] 时间步，与系统规模、动力学和输入编码无关；论文给出普适上界并构造了达到该指数标度的显式储备池。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清"系统规模（量子比特数）"与"记忆能力"之间的关系，回答有噪量子系统究竟能记住多久这一问题，并寻求决定记忆上限的真正资源。

Method: 以每时间步被重新初始化的 k 个输入量子比特作为资源度量；利用新鲜量子比特每步移除的熵刻画动力学，推导出不依赖具体动力学形式的普适记忆上界，并通过构造显式储备池证明该标度可达（紧致性论证）。

Result: 得到最长记忆时间的普适上界 exp[Θ(k)]，且该指数标度可由显式构造的储备池达到；上界仅通过每步熵移除量依赖动力学，因而适用于任意无反馈的马尔可夫噪声动力学，包括无测量纠错方案。

Conclusion: 记忆能力由"新鲜量子比特流量"而非系统尺度决定，构成一条最优资源定律；提升记忆应关注每步的信息刷新/熵移除速率，而非单纯扩大系统规模。

Abstract: Fresh qubits, not size, set how long a noisy quantum system can remember. With $k$ input qubits reinitialized per time step, the longest memory any reservoir can attain under depolarizing noise is $\exp[Θ(k)]$ time steps, whatever its size, dynamics, and input encoding. A universal upper bound and an explicit reservoir that attains the exponential scaling establish this optimal resource law. The bound depends on the dynamics only through the entropy that fresh qubits remove per time step, so it holds for any Markovian noisy dynamics without feedback, including measurement-free error correction.

</details>


### [83] [Strong-to-Weak Spontaneous Symmetry Breaking Transitions on a Quantum Processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26229)
*Zhi-Yuan Wei,Christopher Fechisin,Yu-Xin Wang,Jeet Shah,Tsung-Cheng Lu,Cheng-Ju Lin,Alexey V. Gorshkov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在超导量子处理器上实验解析了强到弱自发对称性破缺（SW-SSB）转变的有限尺寸临界信号，并提出一个仅需两种测量设置、结合量子测量与基于已知退相位信道的经典后处理的协议，重建保真度、Rényi-2与线性关联函数及弦观测量。对幂律关联退相位下的矩阵乘积态，观测到非线性关联函数的有限尺寸交叉与标度塌缩，并进一步获得混合态对称保护拓扑（SPT）相的弦序特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合量子态可表现出无纯态对应的非常规对称性破缺，其中SW-SSB不能由对密度矩阵线性的观测量诊断，因此需要实验上可实现的非线性关联函数测量手段，以刻画其有限尺寸临界行为并探索混合态拓扑序。

Method: 开发量子-经典混合测量协议：将量子测量与基于已知退相位信道的经典后处理结合，以重建非线性混合态关联函数；仅用两种测量设置即可同时重构保真度、Rényi-2关联、线性关联及弦观测量。将协议应用于经受幂律关联退相位的矩阵乘积态，分析有限尺寸交叉与标度塌缩，并映射初始态与信道参数下的SW-SSB转变点。

Result: 在非线性关联函数中观测到有限尺寸交叉与标度塌缩，同时线性关联衰减；获得SW-SSB转变点随初始态与信道参数的分布图。线性与Rényi-2弦观测量的测量显示混合态SPT相特征，且非线性弦序交叉点与独立估计的SW-SSB转变点吻合。

Conclusion: 结果确立了通过量子-经典方法探测SW-SSB临界行为与混合态SPT序的可行途径，并验证了非线性关联函数作为混合态对称破缺诊断工具的实验有效性。

Abstract: Mixed quantum states can exhibit unconventional forms of symmetry breaking without pure-state counterparts. A prominent example is strong-to-weak spontaneous symmetry breaking (SW-SSB), which cannot be diagnosed by observables linear in the density matrix. Here, we experimentally resolve finite-size critical signatures of SW-SSB transitions on a superconducting quantum processor. We develop a measurement protocol that combines quantum measurements with classical processing based on the known dephasing channel to reconstruct nonlinear mixed-state correlators. Using only two measurement settings, the protocol reconstructs fidelity, Rényi-2, and linear correlators, as well as string observables. Applying this approach to matrix-product states subjected to power-law-correlated dephasing, we observe finite-size crossings and scaling collapses in nonlinear correlators, accompanied by decaying linear correlations. We further map the estimated SW-SSB transition points across initial-state and channel parameters. Measurements of linear and Rényi-2 string observables further reveal signatures of mixed-state symmetry-protected topological (SPT) phases, with a nonlinear string-order crossover aligned with an independently estimated SW-SSB transition point. Our results establish a quantum-classical approach to probing SW-SSB critical behavior and mixed-state SPT order.

</details>


### [84] [Universal Quantum Criticality from a Gaussian Variational Manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26246)
*Reyhaneh Khasseh,Fabian Ballar Trigueros,Markus Heyl,M. A. Rajabpour*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出用费米子高斯态系统性地构造量子相变的最优非相互作用描述，在长程横场Ising链上化为自洽Euler方程，给出与数值基准吻合的临界线及连续变化临界性质的解析预测；同时发现高斯态保真度在转变附近最差、非高斯性最强，说明临界性质的准确预测可源于变分描述而非微观波函数近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续相变的普适行为由维度与对称性控制的长波不动点理论刻画，但何种系统能被非相互作用理论捕获这一普适行为并给出解析、准确预测，是核心未解问题。作者希望建立一个系统性框架，为量子相变寻找"最好的"非相互作用费米子描述。

Method: 以费米子高斯态为变分类，构建系统化流程求取量子相变的最优高斯变分描述；将该流程应用于长程横场Ising链，把问题约化为一个自洽Euler方程，由其奇异动量结构提取临界性质。

Result: 自洽Euler方程给出的临界线与独立数值基准高度吻合；其奇异动量结构提供对连续变化临界性质的解析预测。但精确对角化显示，高斯态对精确基态的保真度在相变附近最低、非高斯性最强。

Conclusion: 最优高斯变分描述可在其微观波函数近似最弱之处仍给出准确的临界性质预测，即临界性质的准确性并不依赖于基态波函数的高保真度，揭示了非相互作用长波描述适用性的新机制。

Abstract: Universal behavior at continuous phase transitions is encoded in long-wavelength fixed-point theories controlled by dimensionality and symmetries. It is a central question for which systems this universal behavior and the underlying long-wavelength description can be captured by a noninteracting theory allowing for analytical and accurate predictions. In this work we provide a systematic procedure for obtaining the best noninteracting fermionic description of a quantum phase transition through fermionic Gaussian states. We demonstrate the performance of our procedure for the interacting long-range transverse-field Ising chain. This formulation reduces the problem to a self-consistent Euler equation that yields a critical line in close agreement with independent numerical benchmarks, while its singular momentum structure provides analytic predictions for continuously varying critical properties. Yet exact diagonalization shows that Gaussian-state fidelity to the exact ground state is poorest, and non-Gaussianity strongest, near the transition. Thus, accurate predictions for critical properties can emerge from the Gaussian variational description even where its microscopic wave-function approximation is weakest.

</details>


### [85] [Physics-anchored residual quantum neural networks for cloud-cover parameterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26279)
*Zhuo Wang,Ce Yu,Bin Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在ICON/DYAMOND数据上，将量子神经网络（QNN）改为"离线残差"形式——保留\\XR云量估计并学习其logit的修正量——可使物理空间测试MSE相对直接QNN预测降低38.8%–54.3%，并稳定优于紧凑残差MLP；但更大、更长训练的Grundner-Q1残差模型误差更低，界定了经典参照的上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑量子神经网络已被用于云量参数化，但其预测价值取决于物理知识如何注入以及参照的经典模型强度。作者希望检验"保留已有物理拟合估计、仅学习修正项"这一残差式知识注入方式，能否在有限预算下提升紧凑模拟QNN的预测精度，并明确其相对强经典基线的真实定位。

Method: 采用离线残差建模：保留拟合的\\XR云量估计，在变换后的目标空间中学习其logit的修正量。使用公开的ICON/DYAMOND衍生数据，6输入与8输入两种设置，共10个模型变体，每组3个配对重复、每个重复10,000训练样本；量子线路均采用局部Z测量与无噪声精确期望值；统一30轮训练协议。对照包括直接QNN、紧凑残差MLP，以及规模更大、训练更久的Grundner-Q1适配版本。

Result: 在30轮统一协议下，残差预测相对直接QNN使物理空间测试MSE下降：6输入XYZ与ZZXY线路分别降低54.3%与38.8%，8输入版本分别降低49.2%与38.9%；残差QNN在所有配对重复中均优于所选紧凑残差MLP。但残差Grundner-Q1的平均MSE为0.00778与0.00791，低于残差XYZ的0.01124与0.01082。

Conclusion: 在所测试的预算范围内，残差预测对紧凑模拟QNN是有效的精度提升手段，同时存在由更强经典参照所划定的清晰性能边界；结果不支持量子计算优势，也不代表具备在线气候模式预报技巧。

Abstract: Compact quantum neural networks have been explored for cloud-cover parameterization, but their predictive value depends on how physical knowledge is incorporated and which classical models provide the reference. We investigate an offline residual formulation that retains a fitted \XR\ cloud-cover estimate and learns a correction to its logit in transformed target space. Using released ICON/DYAMOND-derived data, we compare ten model variants in six- and eight-input settings, with three matched replicates and 10,000 training samples per replicate. All quantum circuits use local Z readout and noiseless exact expectation values. Under a common 30-epoch protocol, residual prediction reduced physical-space test mean squared error relative to direct QNN prediction by 54.3\% and 38.8\% for six-input XYZ and ZZXY circuits, and by 49.2\% and 38.9\% for their eight-input versions. Residual QNNs also outperformed the selected compact residual multilayer perceptron in every paired replicate. A larger, longer-trained Grundner-Q1 adaptation nevertheless achieved lower error: residual Q1 reached mean MSEs of 0.00778 and 0.00791, compared with 0.01124 and 0.01082 for residual XYZ. The results support residual prediction for compact simulated QNNs within the tested budget and establish a clear boundary set by stronger classical references. They do not establish computational quantum advantage or online climate-model skill.

</details>


### [86] [Squeezing-type lower bounds on generalized metrological sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26319)
*Irene García-Martínez,Manuel Gessner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文在特定无偏约束下，为一族方差界引入“压缩型”下界，将标准压缩系数推广为广义计量灵敏度，并在可及观测量族内解析识别最优观测量，若观测量任意可及则最优压缩型界精确等于广义计量灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子Cramér-Rao界等现有方差界在有限测量次数及从局域到全局参数估计的过渡中刻画能力不足，缺乏既能保留实验可达结构又能高效表征广义计量灵敏度的界。

Method: 在特定无偏性约束下构建压缩型下界，作为方差界层级体系的推广；将标准压缩系数直接扩展为广义计量灵敏度；并在可及观测量族内解析求解使界最大化的观测量。

Result: 所构建压缩型界保持实验上可达的结构；可在可及观测量族内解析识别最大化该界的观测量；当观测量完全可及时，优化后的压缩型界与精确广义计量灵敏度一致。

Conclusion: 压缩型下界为广义计量灵敏度提供了高效且实验可行的刻画，尤其适用于有限测量次数情形以及局域向全局参数估计的过渡。

Abstract: We introduce squeezing-type lower bounds for a hierarchy of variance bounds under specific unbiasedness constraints, which generalizes the quantum Cramér-Rao bound. Our construction directly extends standard squeezing coefficients to the corresponding generalized metrological sensitivities while retaining their experimentally accessible structure. We show how observables maximizing these bounds can be identified analytically within families of accessible observables. If arbitrary observables are accessible, the optimized squeezing-type bound recovers the exact generalized metrological sensitivity. These bounds provide efficient and experimentally viable characterizations of generalized metrological sensitivities that are especially relevant in finite-shot scenarios and in the transition from local to global parameter estimation.

</details>


### [87] [Higher-order Parity Constraints for Quantum Approximate Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26381)
*Anita Weidinger,Glen Bigan Mbeng,Roeland ter Hoeven,Wolfgang Lechner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: Parity Architecture 的奇偶变换可提升 QAOA 在硬件原生稀疏图上的成功概率，其机制在于高阶算符加速关联传播；在 IBM Heron 处理器上，40 顶点 Signed Max-Cut 实例中奇偶基 QAOA 全部找到最优解，而常规 QAOA 失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Parity Architecture 最初用于量子优化中以克服硬件连通性限制。本文试图说明其作用不止于此：特定奇偶变换能提升 QAOA 在硬件原生、稀疏图优化问题上的成功概率，从而拓展该架构的价值。

Method: 在 3-正则图上研究 Signed Max-Cut 问题；利用奇偶表示引入的高阶算符加速物理器件上的关联传播。QAOA 参数先在小规模训练实例（≤14 顶点）上经典优化，再迁移至更大实例并在 IBM Heron 量子处理器上执行，同时结合误差缓解技术。

Result: 数值模拟表明性能提升源于奇偶表示带来的高阶算符；实验中对至多 40 顶点的 Signed Max-Cut 实例，配合误差缓解的奇偶基 QAOA 在所有 40 顶点实例上均识别出最优解，而相同条件下的常规 QAOA 均失败。

Conclusion: Parity Architecture 不仅解决连通性约束，还可作为提升 QAOA 成功概率的有效手段；高阶算符驱动的关联加速传播为其机制，且在真实超导硬件上具备实用可行性。

Abstract: The Parity Architecture was originally introduced in the context of quantum optimization algorithms to overcome hardware connectivity constraints. Here, we show that its benefits extend beyond this role by identifying specific parity transformations that enhance the success probability of the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA) for optimization problems defined on hardware-native and sparse graphs. Numerical simulations suggest that this enhancement originates from the higher-order operators introduced by the Parity representation, which accelerate the propagation of correlations across the physical device. We investigate this mechanism for the Signed Max-Cut problem on 3-regular graph instances and demonstrate its practical relevance on IBM Heron quantum processors. QAOA parameters are first classically optimized on small training instances containing up to 14 vertices and subsequently transferred to larger instances executed on quantum hardware. We consider Signed Max-Cut instances of up to 40 vertices and find that, together with an error mitigation technique, the parity-based QAOA identifies the optimal solutions for all the considered 40-vertex instances, whereas the conventional QAOA fails under the same conditions.

</details>


### [88] [Two-qubit cryo-CMOS control of a spin register in diamond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26413)
*H. B. van Ommen,N. E. Fakkel,M. van Riggelen,J. Yun,L. A. Enthoven,M. A. B. ElBadry,F. Sebastiano,M. Babaie,T. H. Taminiau*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了在低温恒温器中，将由硅CMOS技术设计的低温CMOS量子比特控制器与金刚石量子器件集成，实现了对NV色心中电子自旋和核自旋的两比特控制。作者利用GST表征单比特门性能并分析限制性噪声，通过动力学解耦构造两比特门，估计电子-核两比特门平均保真度为95(2)%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实用规模量子技术不仅需要性能良好的量子平台，还需要可扩展、高效且满足量子系统设计要求的经典控制硬件。硅CMOS技术成熟且高度可扩展，适合用来设计针对量子系统优化的专用集成电路（ASIC）。作者希望验证低温CMOS控制器驱动色心量子器件的可行性，从而推进高集成度量子器件的发展。

Method: 将低温CMOS量子比特控制器与金刚石量子器件集成在低温恒温器中，对NV色心中的电子自旋和碳核自旋进行两比特控制。使用门集层析（GST）表征两种自旋上的单比特门性能，并分析限制性噪声过程；利用动力学解耦（dynamical decoupling）构造两比特门。

Result: 获得单比特门性能表征及限制噪声分析；电子-核两比特门平均保真度估计为F_2q,avg = 95(2)%。

Conclusion: 低温CMOS驱动的两比特门演示是实现基于色心的高集成度量子器件的关键一步。

Abstract: Utility-scale quantum technologies will not only require a well-performing quantum platform, but also classical control hardware that is scalable, efficient, and meets the design requirements set by the quantum system of interest. One approach is the application of silicon CMOS technology, a highly scalable and mature technology, to design application-specific integrated circuits (ASIC) that are optimized for the quantum system. In this work, we present two-qubit control of electron and nuclear spins in NV centres in diamond, using a cryogenic CMOS qubit controller integrated with a diamond quantum device in a cryostat. Using Gate Set Tomography (GST) we characterize the performance of the single-qubit gates on both electron- and carbon nuclear spins, and analyze the limiting noise processes. We use dynamical decoupling to engineer a two-qubit gate, and estimate $F_\text{2q,avg} = 95(2)\%$ for the electron-nuclear two-qubit gate fidelity. Our demonstration of cryo-CMOS-driven two-qubit gates is a key step towards highly-integrated quantum devices based on colour centres.

</details>


### [89] [Giant Rydberg Excitons Delineated as Relativistic Bound States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26435)
*Mitsuyoshi Takahata,Ikuko Akimoto,Nobuko Naka*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究Cu₂O价带能量-动量关系的非抛物色散，用A·k²/2μ与修正光速描述，并据此给出含有限量子缺陷的Rydberg激子束缚态能量的简洁表达式，指出量子缺陷源于相对论性运动学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cu₂O作为巨型Rydberg态的新型固体宿主受到关注，但其价带色散的非抛物性以及Rydberg激子能级偏离氢原子序列（量子缺陷）的物理起源尚缺乏统一、简洁的描述。

Method: 测量/分析Cu₂O价带的能量-动量关系，采用静质量（0.570倍自由电子质量）与重标度光速（1.6×10⁵ m/s）参数化非抛物色散；类比高能粒子的相对论理论，推导含有限量子缺陷的Rydberg激子束缚态能量的简明解析表达式。

Result: 价带呈现非抛物色散，可由静质量0.570 mₑ与重标度光速1.6×10⁵ m/s刻画；所提出的表达式复现了偏离氢原子序列的激子能级结构，并将量子缺陷归因于相对论性运动学。

Conclusion: 相对论性运动学是Cu₂O中Rydberg激子量子缺陷的物理起源，该简明形式化框架可为后续复杂多体关联研究提供便捷基础。

Abstract: We examined the energy-momentum relationship of the valence band of Cu$_2$O, which has gained interest as an emerging solid host for giant Rydberg states. Our findings reveal a nonparabolic dispersion, described using the rest mass (0.570 times the free electron mass) and rescaled light speed ($1.6\times 10^5$ m/s), which draws parallels with relativistic theory for high-energy particles. We propose a straightforward expression for the bound-state energies of Rydberg excitons with finite quantum defects, whose energy scheme deviates from the hydrogenic series. This formulation identifies relativistic kinematics as the physical origin of quantum defects, offering a streamlined basis for future studies on complex many-body correlations.

</details>


### [90] [The Four-Qubit Hyperbolic Quadric Has Contextuality Degree 315](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26465)
*Zhengyu Li,Amandeep Gupta,Vijay Ganesh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明了关于四量子比特双曲二次曲面 Q⁺(7,2) 的 contextuality degree 猜想：度为 315，且每个最优赋值所违反的 context 集合恰构成一个对偶极空间 DW(5,2)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muller 等人通过启发式搜索提出两个猜想：Q⁺(7,2)（135 点、1575 条线作为 context）的 contextuality degree 为 315，且最优赋值的违反集构成 DW(5,2)。本文旨在严格证明这两个猜想。

Method: 下界用手工短证：每个 doily（GQ(2,2)）含十个 Peres-Mermin 网格、每条线被覆盖四次，故任意赋值至少违反其 15 条线中的 3 条，对 doily 平均得 1575/5=315。等号情形是局部的：赋值最优当且仅当在每个 doily 中违反某个网格的一个 ruling。以此作为 SAT 编码进行枚举，并用群作用分析违反集结构。

Result: 恰有 240 个最优违反集，构成 O⁺(8,2) 的一个轨道，每个都同构于 DW(5,2)。同一论证还给出 N≥4 量子比特、含 L 条线的所有二次曲面的首个下界 ⌈L/5⌉。

Conclusion: 两个猜想均被证明。度数定理在 Lean 4 中完成内核检验，枚举完备性由 DRAT 证明并经经验证的检查器 cake_lpr 认证，达到形式化与机器可验证的严格标准。

Abstract: The contextuality degree of a set of Pauli contexts is the least number of contexts that every noncontextual $\pm1$ assignment violates. For the four-qubit hyperbolic quadric $\mathcal{Q}^{+}(7,2)$, with $135$ points and $1575$ lines as contexts, Muller et al. conjectured from heuristic searches that the degree is $315$ and that the violated contexts of every optimal assignment form a dual polar space $DW(5,2)$. We prove both conjectures. The lower bound has a short proof by hand: every doily ($GQ(2,2)$) of the quadric contains ten Peres-Mermin grids covering each of its lines four times, so every assignment violates at least three of its fifteen lines, and averaging over the doilies gives $1575/5=315$. The equality case is local: an assignment is optimal exactly when it violates one ruling of one grid in every doily. Using this as a SAT encoding, we show that there are exactly $240$ optimal violated sets, one orbit of $\mathrm{O}^+(8,2)$, each isomorphic to $DW(5,2)$. The same argument gives the first lower bounds $\lceil L/5\rceil$ for all quadrics with $L$ lines in $N\ge4$ qubits. The degree theorem is kernel-checked in Lean 4, and the completeness of the enumeration is certified by a DRAT proof checked by the verified checker cake_lpr.

</details>


### [91] [End-to-End Optimization of Partial Fault Tolerance in Neutral-Atom Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26471)
*Stefano Veroni,Aydin Deger,Andrew J. Daley*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究部分容错STAR架构与中性原子平台的协同设计，从物理噪声偏置、逻辑误差建模到应用级性能，验证部分容错方案在中等错误率下可实现更低开销与更低误差的早期量子优势路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完全容错量子计算开销巨大；STAR架构虽降低通用计算成本，但放宽非Clifford操作容错要求，增加了噪声暴露，因而需要与底层硬件深度协同设计以最大化性能与适用性。

Method: 以中性原子平台为载体做STAR架构协同设计：物理层识别171Yb里德堡门的噪声偏置并用误差选择性脉冲优化增强；逻辑层分析内在误差抑制机制，给出领头阶相干逻辑噪声消失；对表面码实现进行详细线路级模拟；针对周期边界Fermi-Hubbard模型设计适配早期实现的模拟线路，并量化部分/完全容错门合成的资源权衡。

Result: 逻辑错误率较此前估计降低一个数量级以上；部分容错相较完全容错在态注入深度上约降低一个数量级，Clifford资源降低更多；在p~1e-3及数千量子比特条件下，部分容错同时实现更低开销与更低错误率。

Conclusion: 对Fermi-Hubbard等类问题，部分容错STAR架构配合中性原子硬件是通向早期量子优势的有竞争力路径。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant (FT) quantum computation offers a pathway to quantum advantage, but its realization is hindered by its significant overhead. The partially FT "STAR" architecture reduces the cost of universal computation at the price of relaxing FT requirements for non-Clifford operations, increasing exposure to hardware noise. Therefore, maximizing its performance and applicability demands careful co-design with the underlying hardware. In this work we investigate the co-design of the STAR architecture with neutral-atom platforms, connecting the underlying atomic physics to logical error modeling and application-level performance. At the physical level, we identify a favorable noise bias in $^{171}\mathrm{Yb}$ Rydberg gates, and show how it can be enhanced through error-selective pulse optimization. At the logical level, we identify an intrinsic error suppression mechanism, yielding vanishing logical coherent noise in leading order. Detailed circuit-level simulations of surface code implementations show more than an order of magnitude reduction in logical error relative to previous estimates. As a concrete target application, we focus on the Fermi-Hubbard model with periodic boundaries, for which we design a simulation circuit particularly suited to early implementations. We quantify the resource tradeoff of partially and fully FT approaches to gate synthesis, with the former showing approximately an order of magnitude reduction in state-injection depth, and an even larger reduction in Clifford resources. Comparing with classical and fully FT approaches, we identify regimes where partial fault tolerance provides both lower overheads and lower error rates. In our estimates, this is accessible with moderate error rates $p\sim 10^{-3}$ and a few thousand qubits, underlining that for these types of problems, partial FT approaches provide a compelling pathway to early quantum advantage.

</details>


### [92] [Majorization for superpositions of nonorthogonal states and the existence of golden states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26483)
*Gökhan Torun*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 澄清叠加资源理论中golden state的存在条件与Löwdin结构，证明鲁棒性与几何度量由等重叠态同时最大化，给出Kraus型自由操作下转换的Gram优序判据，并刻画三参考态golden态存在性及叠加见证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献仅对两个参考态和特殊重叠矩阵给出golden state，且均为Löwdin基下最大相干，但缺乏对其存在性与Löwdin结构的统一解释，也缺少一般转换判据与多参考态存在性条件。

Method: 在相对线性独立非正交参考态的叠加资源理论框架下，将鲁棒性与几何叠加度量映射到对偶基重叠上的经典XY模型基态能量；构造等重叠态；对Kraus型自由操作建立Gram优序充要判据，并用重叠模与Bargmann相位Φ分析三参考态情形。

Result: 鲁棒性与几何度量被同一类与各参考态等重叠的态最大化，其最大值由对偶基重叠的经典XY模型基态能量决定；对所有重叠矩阵，这类且仅这类态在最大无叠加操作下为golden，但不必在Löwdin基下最大相干；Kraus型操作下golden态必须在Löwdin基下最大相干，但三非正交参考态存在golden态当且仅当重叠有公共模s且s≥2cos[(π+|Φ|)/3]。

Conclusion: golden态的存在性与Löwdin最大相干性取决于所选自由操作集合；等重叠态提供完整叠加见证族，表明参考态张成空间上的白噪声除非参考态正交否则是资源。

Abstract: In the resource theory of superposition relative to linearly independent, nonorthogonal reference states, golden states are known for two reference states and for special overlap matrices, and all of them are maximally coherent in the Löwdin basis. We clarify what decides their existence and their Löwdin structure. The robustness and the geometric measure of superposition are maximized by the same states, which overlap equally with every reference state; their maximal values are set by the ground-state energy of a classical $XY$ model on the overlaps of the dual basis. For every overlap matrix, these states, and only these, are golden under the maximal superposition-free operations, but they need not be maximally coherent in the Löwdin basis. For the Kraus-type free operations, we prove a necessary and sufficient criterion, Gram majorization, for converting pure states into targets with nonzero coefficients; it reduces to majorization for orthonormal reference states. It shows that golden states of these operations must be maximally coherent in the Löwdin basis, but that this does not ensure their existence: three reference states that are not orthonormal admit a golden state if and only if their overlaps have a common modulus $s$ and their Bargmann phase $Φ$ obeys $s\geq2\cos[(π+|Φ|)/3]$. Equal-overlap states also yield a complete family of superposition witnesses in the Löwdin basis, which show that white noise on the span of the reference states is a resource unless these states are orthonormal.

</details>

TL;DR: 该文研究正反电子湮灭产生超子-反超子对中的量子导引（steering），给出任意二值投影测量下导引的充要判据与精确边界，并提出削弱统计需求的固定线性见证与角窗优化方案。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在正产生参数与均匀全范围接受下，导引阈值为α≃0.36065，并发现CJWR判据遗漏的导引区域；对J/ψ→ΛΛ̄，导引在全范围平均下存活。统计模型表明在名义z=5并分别优化两个角选择后，所需全范围对样本从CJWR的8.61×10^4降至投影见证的2.80×10^4，减少67.4%，且在产生接受度与衰变不对称参数变化下仍保持优势。

Abstract: In hyperon-antihyperon production through electron-positron annihilation, quantum steering provides a stricter test of nonclassical spin correlations than entanglement. We determine when steering survives angular averaging and optimize angular selections to reduce the sample size needed to resolve it. In the specified production-frame spin convention, we show that windows symmetric about 90 degrees with even production-angle acceptance yield phase-independent Bell-diagonal states. Applying the necessary and sufficient criterion for steering under arbitrary dichotomic projective measurements determines the exact boundary for this family and reveals a steering region missed by the three-setting Cavalcanti-Jones-Wiseman-Reid (CJWR) criterion. For positive production parameters and uniform full-range acceptance, the steering threshold is $α\simeq0.36065$. We derive the covariance of weak-decay angular moments, construct fixed linear witnesses, and optimize the angular window. For $J/ψ\toΛ\barΛ$, steering survives full-range averaging. The statistical model predicts that, after separately optimizing the two angular selections at nominal $z=5$, the required full-range pair sample decreases from $8.61\times10^4$ for CJWR to $2.80\times10^4$ for the projective witness, a 67.4% reduction. This advantage persists under the production-acceptance and analyzing-power variations studied.

</details>


### [94] [Local Measurement Theory for Quantum Cellular Automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26587)
*Tommaso Brambilla,Alessandro Tosini,Paolo Perinotti*

Main category: quant-ph

Method: 以离散时间、因果可逆演化的有限维量子比特格点（量子元胞自动机）为背景；将测量过程描述为局域化完全正映射，组织为由自动机局域动力学诱导的因果锥内有限非自适应局域instrument序列构成的"操作测量协议"；推导其对应初始时间切片上的有效测量及其空间支撑界，并给出测量架构的刻画。

Result: 建立协议诱导的有效测量的空间支撑界与测量架构；在一维最近邻量子比特自动机上构造两步协议，在特定参数下经局域结果粗粒化后实现两相邻格的Bell基投影测量；偏离该点则得到连续形变的Bell测量族，其纠缠与稳定子Rényi非经典性可显式量化。

Abstract: The formulation of quantum measurements compatible with relativistic causality is obstructed by the tension between the locality of observable algebras and the dynamical state transformations induced by measurement processes. We develop a local measurement theory for quantum cellular automata, lattices of finite-dimensional quantum systems with a causal reversible dynamics evolving in discrete time steps. Measurement processes are described by localized completely positive maps and organized into operational measurement protocols, finite non-adaptive sequences of local instruments distributed across the causal cone induced by the automaton local dynamics. Each protocol induces an effective measurement on the initial time slice, whose support is constrained by the causal structure generated by the automaton. Non-local measurement effects therefore arise through causal propagation and composition of local operations, rather than from primitive non-local interventions or instantaneous collapse prescriptions. We establish the corresponding spatial support bounds and characterize the resulting measurement architecture. As an explicit example, we construct a two-step protocol for a one-dimensional nearest-neighbour qubit automaton which, for suitable automaton parameters, realizes a Bell-basis projective measurement on two neighbouring sites after coarse-graining of the local outcomes. Away from this point, the same protocol generates a continuous family of deformed Bell measurements whose entanglement and stabilizer Rényi non-classicality can be quantified explicitly. The framework thus provides a causally consistent operator-algebraic mechanism for synthesizing spatially extended quantum measurements from strictly local operations.

</details>


### [95] [Beyond Fidelity: Resolving Errors in Photonic Seed State Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26603)

Conclusion: 通过误差敏感度框架可系统辨识多光子干涉带来的误差抑制机制，从而设计出对探测器缺陷更鲁棒、成功率更高的线性光学种子态产生方案。

Abstract: An important aspect of many linear optical quantum computing architectures is probabilistically generating small dual-rail qubit entangled states, or seed states. While many schemes to achieve this have been discovered, attention has been primarily focused on minimizing input resources and maximizing success probability rather than establishing a detailed understanding of linear-optical error performance. In this work, we develop a framework for resolving sensitivities to physically distinct errors in linear optical state generation, allowing us to identify precise ways in which these errors affect the properties of the output state. We use this framework to identify seed state generators in which destructive many-photon interference prevents detector loss and detector noise from causing a falsely successful heralding event. We show that this effect suppresses errors, for example cutting the effective loss on the output of particular Bell state generators in half relative to a standard scheme. We also present a new Bell state generator in which both detector loss and detector noise do not affect the quality of the output Bell state to first order, and a 4GHZ generator with >75% higher success probability than a standard circuit. In addition we provide some useful identities, bounds and invariants for studying linear optical seed state generation.

</details>


### [96] [Ground-State Sampling Correspondence between Penalty-Based and Constrained Quantum Annealing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26687)
*Shunta Ide,Shu Tanaka*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文比较了惩罚法与约束量子退火(CQA)在基数约束问题中的基态采样行为，证明了两者在足够大惩罚与长时间退火下条件基态分布趋于一致，并用量子微扰理论解释了这一收敛机制。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子退火对简并基态的采样概率通常不均匀，这种有偏采样取决于驱动哈密顿量在退火末段如何耦合这些简并态。对于带基数约束的组合优化问题，惩罚法与CQA因驱动项不同（横场 vs 全连接XY）而对可行基态的耦合方式存在差异，但其对采样分布的具体影响尚不清晰。

Result: 在所研究的实例中，惩罚量子退火的条件基态分布在足够大惩罚与长退火时间下趋近于CQA的分布；数值模拟显示两者的Jensen–Shannon散度约随惩罚-目标能量比的平方反比减小，与微扰分析一致。该有效哈密顿量的对应关系在相同微扰条件下可推广到等权重固定磁化约束。

Conclusion: 惩罚法与CQA的采样偏置源自共同的物理机制：前者通过虚跃迁诱导的二阶有效相互作用，其主导项恰为投影到可行流形上的CQA驱动。因此二者在微扰极限下采样行为等价，为理解约束量子退火中的采样偏置提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Quantum annealing generally samples degenerate ground states with nonuniform probabilities. This unfair sampling depends on how the driver Hamiltonian couples these states near the end of annealing. For combinatorial optimization problems with a cardinality constraint, the penalty method and constrained quantum annealing (CQA) differ in these couplings. The transverse-field driver of the penalty method does not directly couple feasible ground states, whereas the fully connected XY driver of CQA couples pairs of them related by a single spin exchange. We investigate how this difference affects ground-state sampling, using closed-system simulations of random graph bipartitioning instances together with degenerate perturbation theory. For the instances studied, the conditional ground-state distribution of penalty-based quantum annealing approaches that of CQA at sufficiently large penalties and long annealing times. This convergence can be explained by the effective Hamiltonian of penalty-based quantum annealing, in which virtual single-spin flips to infeasible states generate a second-order interaction proportional to the CQA driver projected onto the feasible ground-state manifold, which is the leading term of the effective Hamiltonian of CQA. In the numerical simulations, the Jensen--Shannon divergence between the conditional ground-state distributions of penalty-based quantum annealing and CQA decreases approximately as the inverse square of the penalty-to-objective energy ratio, consistent with the perturbative analysis. This correspondence between the effective Hamiltonians extends to equal-weight fixed-magnetization constraints under the same perturbative conditions, providing a common mechanism for sampling bias in the two implementations.

</details>

Motivation: 化学反应速率需要高精度关联能差，量子计算可提供路径，但从量子态提取能量所需采样次数过高，成为实际应用的瓶颈；因此需要测量高效的关联能估计方法，并针对电子结构困难的Ru功能化Hf-MOF相关模型进行验证。

Method: 1) 采用"混合估计器"：将量子制备的目标态与稀疏多组态参考态结合，以仅幅度(magnitude-only)的compute-uncompute测量恢复基态能量修正；2) 给出解析方差估计以预测测量精度；3) 在由Ru功能化铪金属有机框架衍生的H₂DBB–Ru模型上进行测试，并比较单行列式公式与优化的哈密顿量项采样；4) 在模拟器与Quantinuum Helios陷阱离子硬件上验证至(10,10)活性空间，包含结合AI辅助态制备与错误检测的50比特电路。

Result: 解析方差估计预测数百次测量即可达1.6 mHa精度，约为单行列式形式的1/100，比优化哈密顿量项采样少约三个数量级；使用近似目标态时，以更简单的态制备即可获得与直接期望值估计相当的精度；模拟器与陷阱离子实验在(10,10)范围内验证有效，包括含AI辅助态制备和错误检测的50比特电路。

Conclusion: 该混合估计框架为困难化学问题中的关联能差提供了一条测量高效的实现路径，兼顾采样成本、态制备复杂度与硬件可行性。

Abstract: Accurate modelling of chemical reaction rates requires precise correlated energy differences. Quantum computing offers a potential route to their determination, but extracting energies from quantum-prepared states can demand prohibitive sampling. We demonstrate measurement-efficient correlated energy estimation using an electronically challenging H$_2$DBB--Ru model derived from a Ru-functionalized hafnium metal-organic framework relevant to radiodynamic and photodynamic therapies. The mixed estimator combines a quantum-prepared target with sparse multiconfiguration references and recovers ground-state corrections using magnitude-only compute-uncompute measurements. Analytical variance estimates predict 1.6 mHa precision with a few hundred shots, about 100 times fewer than the single-determinant formulation and three orders of magnitude fewer than optimized Hamiltonian-term sampling. With approximate targets, it reaches comparable accuracy using simpler state preparation than direct expectation-value estimation. Emulator and trapped-ion experiments on Quantinuum Helios validate the approach up to (10,10), including a 50-qubit circuit combining AI-assisted state preparation with error detection, providing a measurement-efficient route to correlated energy differences for challenging chemical problems.

</details>


### [98] [Quantum Optimal Control Primer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26725)
*Ignacio R. Sola*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 这是一套量子控制博士课程讲义，聚焦量子最优控制及基于梯度与扩展梯度方法的最优控制算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为博士课程提供系统性的量子最优控制教学材料，梳理量子最优控制的核心理论与算法体系。

Method: 以课程讲义形式组织内容，围绕量子最优控制理论，重点讲解基于梯度（gradient）和扩展梯度（extended gradient）方法的最优控制算法。

Result: 形成了在首尔国立大学化学系（2014）及耶拿弗里德里希·席勒大学化学系部分讲授的详细讲义，涵盖量子最优控制及其梯度类算法。

Conclusion: 该讲义可作为量子最优控制领域博士生的教学参考，突出梯度与扩展梯度算法在量子最优控制中的应用。

Abstract: This is a set of detailed notes that are part of a Ph.D. Course on Quantum Control delivered at the Department of Chemistry of Seoul National University in 2014. Part of this course was also taught in the Department of Chemistry of the Friedrich Schiller University Jena. It focuses on quantum optimal control and quantum optimal control algorithms based on the gradient and extended gradient methods.

</details>


### [99] [A General Master Equation for Two Accelerated Detectors: Stationary States and Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26753)
*Riccardo Acquaviva,Paolo Facchi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 推导了同址匀加速双探测器与标量场弱耦合的一般GKLS主方程，并讨论两种耦合下稳态纠缠的加速度依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多针对特定耦合或特定维度/质量情形，缺乏对任意取向、场质量与时空维数统一处理的开放量子系统框架，因而需要建立更一般的GKLS主方程以系统比较不同耦合方案下的稳态纠缠行为。

Method: 对位于Minkowski真空中的两个同址、匀加速二能级探测器与标量场的弱耦合进行开放量子系统建模，保留相干项与耗散项，推导一般化的Gorini-Kossakowski-Lindblad-Sudarshan（GKLS）主方程；在四维无质量场、取向相同条件下，对对称耦合与旋转波耦合两种情形求解定态及其纠缠度（concurrence）。

Result: 在四维无质量场、相同取向情形下：对称耦合给出的concurrence依赖加速度；而旋转波耦合给出的concurrence与加速度无关。

Conclusion: GKLS框架可统一描述任意取向、场质量与维数下匀加速探测器的相干与耗散效应；纠缠的加速度依赖性强烈依赖于耦合方式，旋转波耦合可屏蔽加速度对稳态纠缠的影响，这对理解加速参考系中纠缠退相干机制具有参考意义。

Abstract: We derive a general Gorini-Kossakowski-Lindblad-Sudarshan master equation for two co-located, uniformly accelerated two-level detectors weakly coupled to scalar fields in the Minkowski vacuum, retaining coherent and dissipative contributions for arbitrary orientations, field masses, and spacetime dimensions. For equal orientations, we determine stationary states and their entanglement for two couplings to massless fields in four dimensions: symmetric coupling yields acceleration-dependent concurrence, whereas rotating-wave coupling gives acceleration-independent concurrence.

</details>


### [100] [PPT-assisted capacities of bosonic Gaussian channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26783)
*Matthew Barber,Stefano Pirandola*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文精确确定了热损耗、热放大与加性高斯噪声三种基本玻色信道在PPT保持操作辅助下的量子通信与纠缠分发容量，并在能量约束下给出各输入平均能量下的精确容量，从而解决了一类玻色信道容量问题并建立了判定LOCC可达性的精确基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LOCC辅助下的玻色信道容量求解困难，而PPT保持操作比LOCC更强、又不能单独产生可提纯纠缠，因此以其为辅助资源可为容量问题提供可解且具基准意义的框架。

Method: 在完全保持正部分转置（PPT）的操作辅助下，针对热损耗、热放大和加性高斯噪声三类玻色信道推导量子通信与纠缠分发容量的精确上下界，并将结果推广到带平均输入能量约束的情形。

Result: 热损耗信道的无约束容量在全部非纠缠破坏区域内与PLOB界一致；热放大与加性噪声信道亦达到相应上界；三类信道在每个给定平均输入能量下均获得精确容量。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了一类PPT辅助下的玻色信道容量问题，并为判断相同通信速率能否仅靠LOCC实现提供了精确判据。

Abstract: We determine the exact quantum communication and entanglement-distribution capacities of three fundamental bosonic channels, namely thermal loss, thermal amplification and additive Gaussian noise, assisted by operations that completely preserve positive partial transpose (PPT). These operations are more powerful than local operations and classical communication (LOCC), but cannot generate distillable entanglement on their own. For thermal loss, the unconstrained capacity coincides with the Pirandola-Laurenza-Ottaviani-Banchi (PLOB) bound throughout the non-entanglement-breaking regime. The corresponding upper bounds are also attained for thermal amplification and additive noise. We extend these results to energy-constrained communication, establishing exact capacities for all three channels at every prescribed mean input energy. Our results solve a class of bosonic capacity problems under PPT assistance and establish exact benchmarks for determining whether the same communication rates can be achieved via LOCC alone.

</details>


### [101] [High-Accuracy Propagation-Assisted Local Decoding for Fast Transversal Clifford Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26784)
*Mark Webber,Simanraj Sadana,Matthew A. Lane,Lucy Jade Robson,Anthony O'Rourke*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并基准测试迭代重解码方案（IRS），将采样得到的局部解码器纳入“传播辅助局部（PAL）”框架，在快速横向Clifford电路上实现高精度关联解码，逻辑错误率显著优于ghost协议、LOM与可靠逻辑Pauli乘积解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速横向Clifford电路中的关联错误传播使传统全局超图解码代价高、精度不足；现有ghost协议、逻辑可观测量匹配（LOM）、可靠逻辑Pauli乘积解码等在深电路与GHZ制备等场景下误差抑制有限，需要一种高效且可扩展的局部解码方案。

Method: 基于Wan等人的方案构建IRS：在内存等价探测器误差模型（DEM）上交替执行局部MWPM解码、Pauli帧传播与跨横向CNOT层的探测器更新，使局部误差估计与全局探测器记录一致；引入先验信息DEM刻画被遗漏的传播错误概率；用零权重物理Pauli机制推断与传播初始不确定的稳定子结果；设置迭代上限，并研究综合征提取缓冲区规模。

Result: 深随机Clifford基准下，d=11、缓冲区匹配时IRS逻辑失效率为ghost协议的约1/4、LOM的1/15；三块GHZ制备中为可靠逻辑Pauli乘积解码的1/2（d=19、⌈d/2⌉轮全初始化时达1/25）；重复逻辑S门较LOM低约4–10倍；误差率在少数迭代后饱和，⌊d/4⌋量级缓冲区即可实现距离相关抑制。

Conclusion: IRS构成PAL框架下的有效实例，通过局部解码器间消息交换替代全局超图解码，实现高精度、可迭代封顶的关联解码；方法可推广至其他局部解码器与CSS码。

Abstract: We benchmark the Iterative Redecode Scheme (IRS) on fast transversal Clifford circuits, achieving high-accuracy correlated decoding using local memory-style detectors and decoders. On a deep random Clifford benchmark, IRS yields approximately $4\times$ lower logical failure rates than the ghost protocol at $d=11$ with matched buffers, and $15\times$ lower rates than logical observable matching (LOM). In three-block GHZ preparation, IRS achieves $2\times$ lower logical error rates than reliable logical Pauli-product decoding with matched initialisation, improving to $25\times$ at $d=19$ with $\lceil d/2\rceil$ full initialisation rounds. We place IRS, which builds on Wan et al.'s scheme, within a broader propagation-assisted local (PAL) framework, where local decoders exchange messages rather than decoding a global hypergraph. IRS alternates local minimum-weight perfect matching (MWPM) on memory-equivalent detector error models (DEMs) with Pauli-frame propagation and detector updates across transversal CNOT layers, seeking local error estimates consistent with the global detector record. Prior-informed DEMs account for probabilities of propagated errors missing from the memory-equivalent model, improving initial decoding and reducing logical error rates. Zero-weight physical Pauli mechanisms enable inference and propagation of initially indeterminate stabilizer outcomes. Error rates saturate after a few iterations even for deep, adversarial alternating-tCNOT circuits, allowing iteration caps with negligible loss of accuracy. We find syndrome-extraction buffers of order $\lfloor d/4\rfloor$ sufficient for distance-dependent error suppression in the demanding regimes studied. The approach extends to other local decoders and CSS codes; for repeated logical $S$ gates, IRS achieves approximately $4$ to $10\times$ lower logical error rates than LOM, depending on initialisation.

</details>


### [102] [Correlated decoding loses the surface code's effective distance below threshold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26785)
*César Hernando de la Fuente,Joonas Majaniemi,Evert van Nieuwenburg,Felix Frohnert*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 相关解码通过在一个解码子图上依据另一子图的纠错结果对边权重重加权，从而改进匹配与聚类表面码解码器，但标准重加权规则会在距离-d码本应纠正的size-(d-1)/2故障配置上失效，恶化阈值以下的错误抑制；采用正则化重加权可恢复标称距离标度并带来明确的相关解码增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相关解码已被用于提升表面码匹配与聚类解码器的性能，但现有标准重加权规则缺乏对失效机制的刻画，可能损害码的距离标度与深度阈值下的错误抑制能力，因此需要揭示其缺陷并提出修正方案。

Method: 分析相关解码中一个解码子图的纠错结果如何为另一子图提供条件先验并重加权其边权重；构造size-(d-1)/2的故障配置检验距离-d码的可纠正性；在电路级噪声模型下分析单条首轮边对多条相关边的折扣效应及其对独立故障假设的违背；提出正则化重加权规则以约束过度折扣。

Result: 标准重加权会使相关解码器在size-(d-1)/2故障配置上失败，使深度阈值以下的错误抑制显著恶化；在电路级噪声下，单条首轮边即可对多条相关边过度折扣，即联合出现需要额外独立故障，却导致解码器偏向过度折扣的纠错解；正则化重加权恢复了标称的距离标度，并展现出清晰的相关解码优势。

Conclusion: 标准相关重加权在电路级噪声下存在系统性缺陷，会破坏距离-d码应有的纠错能力；通过正则化重加权规则可修复该问题，既恢复距离标度，又保留并凸显相关解码相对独立解码的性能优势。

Abstract: Correlated decoding improves matching and clustering surface-code decoders by reweighting one decoding subgraph conditioned on the other's correction. We show that standard reweighting can cause correlated decoders to fail on size-$(d-1)/2$ fault configurations that a distance-$d$ code should correct, reducing error suppression deep below threshold. Under circuit-level noise, a single first-pass edge can discount several correlated edges, even though their joint occurrence requires additional independent faults, so the decoder favors an over-discounted correction. Regularizing the reweighting rule restores the nominal distance scaling and a clear correlated-decoding advantage.

</details>


### [103] [Fast Hubbard-Thouless Pumping with Local Counterdiabatic Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26786)
*Alexander Chansky*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究Rice-Mele模型中Thouless泵浦的精确CD项非局域性问题：无相互作用时变分近邻近似有效；有相互作用时无限温变分作用失效，改用按跃迁能量分辨的近邻流并配合基态加权作用，得到14系数局域拟设，使泵浦在T=2/t、1/t下保持量子化且不随环尺寸退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速协议的激发需由反绝热（CD）驱动抑制，但多体与拓扑系统中精确CD项通常非局域且多体，难以实现；作者欲考察该阻碍在Thouless泵浦中的具体表现及可实现的局域近似方案。

Method: 以Rice-Mele模型为平台，先研究无相互作用费米子（精确CD非局域，Sels-Polkovnikov变分框架给出准确近邻近似）；再研究含自旋、在位与近邻排斥的链，在两种相互作用竞争稳定量子化泵浦的区域，分析无限温变分作用的两参数近邻项；诊断出精确多体规范势振幅依赖跃迁所耗相互作用能，据此按能量分辨近邻流并用基态加权作用定系数。

Result: 无相互作用情形近邻变分近似精度良好；相互作用情形下无限温作用仅惩罚CD振幅，两参数近邻项在周期从50/t缩至2/t时泵浦崩溃；按能量分辨并基态加权定系数的14系数局域拟设在T=2/t与1/t下保持泵浦量子化，且8与12格环上每格精度不退化。

Conclusion: 相互作用泵浦CD局域化的关键不在于简单惩罚振幅，而在于刻画规范势对跃迁相互作用能的依赖；按能量分辨近邻流并基态加权定系数可获得紧凑、尺寸稳定的局域CD拟设。

Abstract: Counterdiabatic (CD) driving suppresses the excitations of fast protocols by adding an auxiliary Hamiltonian, but in many-body and topological systems the exact CD term is generally non-local and multi-body. We study this obstruction for Thouless pumping in the Rice-Mele model, first without and then with interactions. For noninteracting fermions, the exact CD term of the pump is non-local, but the variational framework of Sels and Polkovnikov yields accurate nearest-neighbor approximations. For the spinful Rice-Mele chain with onsite and nearest-neighbor repulsion, in the regime where the competition of the two interactions stabilizes quantized pumping, this approach fails: the infinite-temperature variational action only penalizes the CD amplitudes, and the resulting two-parameter nearest-neighbor term does not prevent the pump from breaking down when the cycle time is reduced from $50/t$ to $2/t$. We trace the failure to the structure of the exact many-body gauge potential, whose amplitude for each hop depends on the interaction energy that the hop costs. Resolving the nearest-neighbor currents by this energy, and fixing the coefficients with a ground-state-weighted action, gives a local ansatz with 14 coefficients that keeps the interacting pump quantized for cycle times $T=2/t$ and $T=1/t$, with an accuracy per site that does not degrade between rings of 8 and 12 sites.

</details>


### [104] [Supervised Learning with Quantum Hamiltonian Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26833)
*Riccardo Molteni,Minjun Jeon,Paolo Braccia,Abhinav Deshpande,Vedran Dunjko,Liang Jiang,Su-un Lee,Swarnadeep Majumder,Javier Robledo Moreno,Parthasarathy Suryanarayanan,Kunal Sharma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出"量子哈密顿量嵌入"(Hamiltonian embeddings)监督学习框架，将训练数据编码为单个含参有效哈密顿量，其近似基态定义分类规则；在梯度估计、泛化保证、经典不可模拟性与IBM Heron处理器上的大规模实验方面给出系统结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算被认为有望在机器学习上带来计算优势，但具备可证明的性能、可训练性与高效推理保证的通用量子机器学习模型仍然有限，因此需要一个同时具备理论保证与实验可扩展性的新框架。

Method: 将经典训练数据集编码为单个数据依赖的参数化有效哈密顿量；训练即优化该哈密顿量的参数，分类规则由其近似基态决定；用某个量子算法近似基态后，可用经典表示或量子表示执行推理；并给出高效的梯度估计方法。

Result: 建立了高效的梯度估计方法，证明了良好的泛化保证；在标准密码学假设下表明哈密顿量嵌入可实现高效经典算法无法达到的假设函数；在IBM Heron处理器上使用标准基准数据集完成了大规模演示。

Conclusion: 哈密顿量嵌入构成量子机器学习的新框架，兼具理论保证与在量子硬件上开展大规模实验研究的可行路径。

Abstract: Machine learning is widely regarded as a promising application in which quantum computing could offer a computational advantage. However, general quantum machine learning models with provable guarantees on performance, trainability, and efficient inference remain limited. In this work, we introduce quantum Hamiltonian embeddings, a supervised learning framework in which a classical training dataset is encoded into a single effective, parameterized Hamiltonian. Training optimizes the parameters of this data-dependent Hamiltonian, whose approximate ground state defines the classification rule. Once the ground state has been approximated using a quantum algorithm, inference can be performed either classically using a classical representation of the learned state, or with a quantum representation of the state. We establish efficient methods for gradient estimation and prove favorable generalization guarantees. Furthermore, under standard cryptographic assumptions, we show that Hamiltonian embeddings can realize hypothesis functions beyond the reach of efficient classical algorithms. We then demonstrate the framework at scale on IBM Heron processors using a standard benchmark dataset. Together, these results establish Hamiltonian embeddings as a new framework for quantum machine learning, combining theoretical guarantees with a path for experimental investigations at scale on quantum hardware.

</details>


### [105] [Quantum algorithms for optimal control: large-scale finite-horizon linear-quadratic regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26843)
*Xiajie Huang,Cristina Álvarez Yuste,Nikita Guseynov,Nana Liu,Javier González-Conde*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于量子线性系统求解器的有限时域连续时间LQR量子算法，通过多打靶重构状态-协态最优性方程，并证明其条件数与时域长度无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典方法求解LQR需解非线性矩阵Riccati方程，对于大系统与长控制时域代价高昂，作者希望利用量子算法在系统规模与控制时长上实现多项式对数级加速。

Method: 直接处理状态-协态最优性方程，将LQR重构为多打靶线性系统，把初末条件并入单一线性系统；在可镇定性、可检测性假设下证明条件数界与时间域无关，进而调用量子线性系统子程序求解。

Result: 对给定初始状态，量子算法返回最优代价与指定时刻最优控制输入的经典估计，无需构造Riccati解算子；在稀疏矩阵块编码、初态制备oracle与固定控制输入数下，查询与门复杂度随系统规模和控制时长呈多对数增长，误差依赖为逆线性（至多对数因子）。对带两端控制的阻尼质量-弹簧链给出了与规模、时长一致的界，数值实验验证了分析。

Conclusion: 有限时域LQR可通过多打靶线性系统与量子线性求解器高效求解，条件数与时域无关是关键优势，为大规模长时域最优控制提供了潜在量子加速路径。

Abstract: We develop quantum algorithms for optimal control over a prescribed finite time interval (finite horizon), focusing on continuous-time linear-quadratic regulation (LQR). This foundational control problem often requires solving a nonlinear matrix Riccati equation, which can be costly for large systems and long control durations on conventional classical solvers. We instead work directly with the state-costate optimality equations, reformulating the LQR problem as a multiple-shooting linear system. Importantly, we prove a condition-number bound for the system that is independent of the horizon, under stabilizability and detectability assumptions. By incorporating the initial and final conditions into a single linear system, we can solve the subsequent linear system more efficiently with a quantum linear systems subroutine. For a given initial state, our quantum algorithms return classical estimates of the optimal cost and the optimal control inputs at a selected time, without constructing a Riccati solution operator. Given sparse-matrix block-encodings, an initial-state preparation oracle and a fixed number of control inputs, the query and gate complexities scale polylogarithmically with system size and control duration, provided that sparsity, relevant norms, and stability parameters are bounded. The dependence on the target error is inverse-linear up to logarithmic factors. We establish the required bounds uniformly in system size and duration for a family of damped mass-spring chains with two endpoint controls. Numerical experiments support the analysis.

</details>
